 161 def _build_speaker_roster(
 162     host_name: str,
 163     guest_name: str = "",
 164     participant_names: Optional[List[str]] = None
 165 ) -> List[str]:
 166     """Builds deduplicated list of up to 5 speaker names using exact user-provided names."""
 167     clean_host = (host_name or "Host").strip()
 168     if not clean_host or clean_host.upper() in ("ABHIGOWDA819761", "ABHISHEK", "DEFAULT"):
 169         clean_host = "Host"
 170 
 171     roster = [clean_host]
 172     raw_candidates: List[str] = []
 173 
 174     if participant_names:
 175         for item in participant_names:
 176             if isinstance(item, str):
 177                 raw_candidates.extend([x.strip() for x in item.split(",") if x.strip()])
 178     if guest_name:
 179         raw_candidates.extend([x.strip() for x in guest_name.split(",") if x.strip()])
 180 
 181     for name in raw_candidates:
 182         if (
 183             name
 184             and name.upper() not in ("ABHIGOWDA819761", "ABHISHEK", "DEFAULT")
 185             and name.lower() not in [r.lower() for r in roster]
 186         ):
 187             roster.append(name)
 188         if len(roster) >= 5:
 189             break
 190 
 191     if len(roster) == 1:
 192         roster.append("Participant 1")
 193     return roster[:5]
 194 
 195 
 196 def _parse_robust_json(text: str) -> Optional[Any]:
 197     if not text:
 198         return None
 199     cleaned = text.strip()
 200     cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE)
 201     cleaned = re.sub(r"```$", "", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE).strip()
 202     try:
 203         return json.loads(cleaned)
 204     except Exception:
 205         pass
 206 
 207     fixed = re.sub(r',\s*([\]\}])', r'\1', cleaned)
 208     try:
 209         return json.loads(fixed)
 210     except Exception:
 211         pass
 212 
 213     m = re.search(r'(\{[\s\S]*\}|\[[\s\S]*\])', cleaned)
 214     if m:
 215         candidate = m.group(1)
 216         try:
 217             return json.loads(candidate)
 218         except Exception:
 219             try:
 220                 fixed_cand = re.sub(r',\s*([\]\}])', r'\1', candidate)
 221                 return json.loads(fixed_cand)
 222             except Exception:
 223                 pass
 224     return None
 225

 227 async def transcribe_audio(
 228     wav_path: str,
 229     host_name: str = "Host",
 230     guest_name: str = "Participant 1",
 231     groq_api_key: Optional[str] = None,
 232     participant_names: Optional[List[str]] = None,
 233     gemini_api_key: Optional[str] = None
 234 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 235     """
 236     100% Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio Diarization & Verbatim Transcription:
 237     Sends the audio directly to Gemini 2.5 Flash so Gemini listens to the distinct voices,
 238     separates 'Who Spoke What' across up to 5 user-entered speaker names, and extracts
 239     every spoken turn with timestamps and filler words.
 240     """
 241     speaker_roster = _build_speaker_roster(host_name, guest_name, participant_names)
 242     clean_host = speaker_roster[0]
 243     primary_key, secondary_key = get_gemini_api_keys(override_primary=gemini_api_key or groq_api_key)
 244 
 245     if not primary_key:
 246         raise RuntimeError("GEMINI_API_KEY_PRIMARY (or GEMINI_API_KEY) is missing in backend/.env.")
 247 
 248     # Dual-Key Work-Splitting: prefer primary key for heavy audio, fallback to secondary key
 249     keys_to_try = [primary_key]
 250     if secondary_key and secondary_key != primary_key:
 251         keys_to_try.append(secondary_key)
 252 
 253     upload_path, mime_type = _prepare_gemini_audio(wav_path)
 254     try:
 255         with open(upload_path, "rb") as f:
 256             audio_b64 = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode("utf-8")
 257 
 258         prompt = f"""You are an expert Multimodal Audio Diarization and Verbatim Transcription Engine for 'Guardian Eye AI'.
 259 Listen carefully to the attached meeting/interview audio recording and transcribe every spoken turn verbatim, identifying exactly WHO spoke WHAT.
 260 
 261 === REGISTERED MEETING PARTICIPANTS (UP TO 5) ===
 262 Host / Moderator / Interviewer: "{clean_host}"
 263 Full Speaker Roster (in order): {json.dumps(speaker_roster)}
 264 
 265 === STRICT DIARIZATION & TRANSCRIPTION RULES ===
 266 1. Listen to the distinct vocal pitch, timbre, cadence, and conversational turn-taking (questions vs. answers, greetings, and name mentions) in the audio.
 267 2. Attribute every spoken turn strictly to the matching speaker name from {json.dumps(speaker_roster)}:
 268    - Assign the primary interviewer / moderator / host voice to "{clean_host}".
 269    - Assign each distinct participant / candidate / guest voice consistently to the remaining names in {json.dumps(speaker_roster)}.
 270    - If a speaker is addressed by name in the audio, bind that voice to that exact name.
 271 3. Transcribe 100% verbatim in English (or transliterated Roman script if another language is spoken), preserving natural filler words ("um", "uh", "like", "you know") so our communication fluency scorer can count them accurately.
 272 4. Provide accurate `start` and `end` timestamps in seconds (float) for every turn.
 273 
 274 Return ONLY valid JSON matching this exact schema:
 275 {{
 276   "segments": [
 277     {{
 278       "start": 0.0,
 279       "end": 4.2,
 280       "speaker": "{clean_host}",
 281       "text": "Exact verbatim words spoken in this turn."
 282     }}
 283   ]
 284 }}"""
 285 
 286         payload = {
 287             "contents": [{
 288                 "parts": [
 289                     {"inline_data": {"mime_type": mime_type, "data": audio_b64}},
 290                     {"text": prompt}
 291                 ]
 292             }],
 293             "generationConfig": {
 294                 "temperature": 0.0,
 295                 "responseMimeType": "application/json"
 296             }
 297         }
 298 
 299         import httpx
 300         raw_segments: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
 301         used_model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
 302         models_to_try = ("gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash", "gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-2.0-flash", "gemini-1.5-flash")
 303 
 304         for key_candidate in keys_to_try:
 305             if raw_segments:
 306                 break
 307             for model_id in models_to_try:
 308                 try:
 309                     url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model_id}:generateContent?key={key_candidate}"
 310                     async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=180.0) as client:
 311                         resp = await client.post(url, json=payload)
 312                         if resp.status_code == 200:
 313                             resp_json = resp.json()
 314                             candidates = resp_json.get("candidates", [])
 315                             if candidates:
 316                                 text_content = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [{}])[0].get("text", "")
 317                                 parsed = _parse_robust_json(text_content)
 318                                 segs = parsed.get("segments", []) if isinstance(parsed, dict) else (parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else [])
 319                                 if segs:
 320                                     raw_segments = segs
 321                                     used_model = f"{model_id} (Native Audio)"
 322                                     print(f"[Gemini Audio Diarizer] {model_id} extracted {len(raw_segments)} speaker turns using {'Primary' if key_candidate == primary_key else 'Secondary'} Key.")
 323                                     break
 324                         else:
 325                             print(f"[Gemini Audio {model_id} Error] HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:250]}")
 326                 except Exception as e:
 327                     print(f"[Gemini Audio {model_id} Exception] {e}")
 328 
 329         # Local whisper fallback if Gemini network call failed
 330         if not raw_segments:
 331             try:
 332                 from diarizer import get_whisper_model
 333                 import asyncio
 334                 print("[Gemini Audio Fallback] Running local audio diarization & transcription on WAV...")
 335                 model = get_whisper_model("base")
 336                 if model is not None:
 337                     res = await asyncio.to_thread(model.transcribe, wav_path, fp16=False, language="en")
 338                     raw_segments = res.get("segments", [])
 339                     used_model = "local-whisper-fallback"
 340             except Exception as local_err:
 341                 print(f"[Local Transcription Notice] {local_err}")
 342 
 343         dialogue_turns: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
 344         dialogue_lines: List[str] = []
 345 
 346         for idx, seg in enumerate(raw_segments):
 347             text = (seg.get("text") or "").strip()
 348             if not text:
 349                 continue
 350             start = float(seg.get("start", idx * 3.5))
 351             end = float(seg.get("end", start + max(1.5, len(text.split()) * 0.4)))
 352             ts = format_sec(start)
 353 
 354             raw_spk = (seg.get("speaker") or clean_host).strip()
 355             matched_spk = raw_spk
 356             for r_name in speaker_roster:
 357                 if r_name.lower() == raw_spk.lower() or r_name.lower() in raw_spk.lower():
 358                     matched_spk = r_name
 359                     break
 360             if matched_spk not in speaker_roster:
 361                 # Map generic Speaker 0 / Speaker 1 labels to roster
 362                 num_match = re.search(r'(\d+)', raw_spk)
 363                 if num_match:
 364                     spk_idx = int(num_match.group(1))
 365                     matched_spk = speaker_roster[spk_idx % len(speaker_roster)]
 366                 else:
 367                     matched_spk = speaker_roster[idx % len(speaker_roster)]
 368 
 369             is_host = (matched_spk.lower() == clean_host.lower())
 370             turn_idx = len(dialogue_turns)
 371             dialogue_turns.append({
 372                 "id": f"turn_{turn_idx}",
 373                 "speaker": matched_spk,
 374                 "role": "Host" if is_host else "Participant",
 375                 "isHost": is_host,
 376                 "text": text,
 377                 "timestamp": ts,
 378                 "start": round(start, 2),
 379                 "end": round(end, 2)
 380             })
 381             dialogue_lines.append(f"{matched_spk}: {text}")
 382 
 383         formatted_transcript = "\n".join(dialogue_lines)
 384         return {
 385             "model": used_model,
 386             "rawTranscript": formatted_transcript,
 387             "dialogueTurns": dialogue_turns,
 388             "text": formatted_transcript,
 389             "segments": dialogue_turns,
 390             "groqSegments": dialogue_turns,
 391             "speakerRoster": speaker_roster
 392         }
 393     finally:
 394         if upload_path != wav_path and os.path.exists(upload_path):
 395             try:
 396                 os.remove(upload_path)
 397             except Exception:
 398                 pass
 399 
 400 
 401 async def generate_gemini_summary(raw_transcript: str, gemini_api_key: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
 402     """Uses Gemini 2.5 Flash to generate a concise executive summary with dual-key load splitting."""
 403     primary_key, secondary_key = get_gemini_api_keys(override_secondary=gemini_api_key)
 404     keys_to_try = [k for k in [secondary_key, primary_key] if k]
 405     if not keys_to_try or not raw_transcript.strip():
 406         return ""
 407     try:
 408         import httpx
 409         payload = {
 410             "contents": [{
 411                 "parts": [{
 412                     "text": f"Generate a concise 2-3 sentence executive summary strictly based on the verbatim words spoken in this meeting transcript:\n\n{raw_transcript}"
 413                 }]
 414             }],
 415             "generationConfig": {"temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 200}
 416         }
 417         for key_candidate in keys_to_try:
 418             for model_id in ("gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash", "gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-2.0-flash"):
 419                 url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model_id}:generateContent?key={key_candidate}"
 420                 try:
 421                     async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as client:
 422                         resp = await client.post(url, json=payload)
 423                         if resp.status_code == 200:
 424                             candidates = resp.json().get("candidates", [])
 425                             if candidates:
 426                                 return candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [{}])[0].get("text", "").strip()
 427                 except Exception:
 428                     pass
 429     except Exception as e:
 430         print(f"[Gemini Summary Notice] {e}")
 431     return ""
 432 
 433 # Backward-compatibility alias
 434 generate_groq_summary = generate_gemini_summary
 435 
 436 
 437 def extract_speaker_linguistic_metrics(
 438     segments: List[Dict],
 439     speaker_roster: List[str]
 440 ) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
 441     """Computes objective WPM, filler words, word count, char count, and hesitation pauses for up to 5 speakers."""
 442     metrics: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
 443     for spk in speaker_roster:
 444         metrics[spk] = {
 445             "words": 0, "chars": 0, "segments": 0, "fillers": 0,
 446             "duration_sec": 0.0, "wpm": 0, "long_pauses": 0, "filler_rate_pct": 0.0
 447         }
 448 
 449     filler_regex = re.compile(
 450         r'\b(uh|um|uhm|ah|er|erm|like|you know|sort of|kind of|basically|literally|i mean)\b',
 451         re.IGNORECASE
 452     )
 453     sound_artifacts_regex = re.compile(
 454         r'\[(applause|music|laughter|silence|whispering|cough|throat clearing)\]',
 455         re.IGNORECASE
 456     )
 457     prev_end_by_speaker: Dict[str, float] = {}
 458 
 459     for seg in (segments or []):
 460         text = seg.get("text", "")
 461         cleaned = sound_artifacts_regex.sub("", text).strip()
 462         if not cleaned:
 463             continue
 464 
 465         raw_spk = (seg.get("speaker") or seg.get("speakerName") or speaker_roster[0]).strip()
 466         target_key = None
 467         for r_spk in speaker_roster:
 468             if raw_spk.lower() == r_spk.lower() or r_spk.lower() in raw_spk.lower():
 469                 target_key = r_spk
 470                 break
 471         if not target_key:
 472             target_key = raw_spk
 473             if target_key not in metrics:
 474                 metrics[target_key] = {
 475                     "words": 0, "chars": 0, "segments": 0, "fillers": 0,
 476                     "duration_sec": 0.0, "wpm": 0, "long_pauses": 0, "filler_rate_pct": 0.0
 477                 }
 478 
 479         words = len(cleaned.split())
 480         chars = len(cleaned)
 481         fillers = len(filler_regex.findall(cleaned))
 482         start_t = float(seg.get("start", 0.0))
 483         end_t = float(seg.get("end", start_t + max(1.0, words * 0.4)))
 484         seg_dur = max(0.5, end_t - start_t)
 485 
 486         metrics[target_key]["words"] += words
 487         metrics[target_key]["chars"] += chars
 488         metrics[target_key]["segments"] += 1
 489         metrics[target_key]["fillers"] += fillers
 490         metrics[target_key]["duration_sec"] += seg_dur
 491 
 492         if target_key in prev_end_by_speaker:
 493             gap = start_t - prev_end_by_speaker[target_key]
 494             if 1.8 <= gap <= 8.0:
 495                 metrics[target_key]["long_pauses"] += 1
 496         prev_end_by_speaker[target_key] = end_t
 497 
 498     for spk_key, data in metrics.items():
 499         dur = data["duration_sec"]
 500         w = data["words"]
 501         data["wpm"] = round((w / dur) * 60) if dur > 0 else 0
 502         data["filler_rate_pct"] = round((data["fillers"] / max(w, 1)) * 100.0, 1)
 503 
 504     return metrics
 505 
 506 
 507 async def analyze_with_gemini(
 508     raw_transcript: str,
 509     dialogue_turns: List[Dict],
 510     host_name: str = "Host",
 511     guest_name: str = "Participant 1",
 512     gemini_api_key: Optional[str] = None,
 513     segments: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
 514     participant_names: Optional[List[str]] = None
 515 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 516     """
 517     Uses Google Gemini 2.5 Flash to strictly and objectively score every active speaker (up to 5)
 518     on a 1–10 scale, generate the executive summary, and perform Q&A Fact-Checking.
 519     """
 520     primary_key, secondary_key = get_gemini_api_keys(override_secondary=gemini_api_key)
 521     # Dual-Key Work-Splitting: prefer Secondary key for scoring/fact-check, failover to Primary
 522     keys_to_try = [secondary_key] if secondary_key else []
 523     if primary_key and primary_key not in keys_to_try:
 524         keys_to_try.append(primary_key)
 525 
 526     speaker_roster = _build_speaker_roster(host_name, guest_name, participant_names)
 527     clean_host = speaker_roster[0]
 528 
 529     analysis_segments = dialogue_turns if (dialogue_turns and len(dialogue_turns) > 0) else (segments or [])
 530     linguistic_metrics = extract_speaker_linguistic_metrics(analysis_segments, speaker_roster)
 531 
 532     if keys_to_try and len(analysis_segments) > 0:
 533         import httpx
 534 
 535         prompt = f"""You are a STRICT, UNBIASED Communication Auditor and Fact-Checking Engine for 'Guardian Eye AI'.
 536 Evaluate each active speaker in the meeting transcript below using the objective linguistic telemetry. DO NOT inflate scores or give polite default 7/10 or 8/10 ratings.
 537 
 538 === REGISTERED PARTICIPANTS (UP TO 5) ===
 539 Host: "{clean_host}"
 540 All Registered Speakers: {json.dumps(speaker_roster)}
 541 
 542 === OBJECTIVE LINGUISTIC TELEMETRY PER SPEAKER ===
 543 {json.dumps(linguistic_metrics, indent=2)}
 544 
 545 === STRICT, UNBIASED 1-TO-10 GRADING RUBRIC ===
 546 - 5/10 is the baseline average score.
 547 - 9 to 10 (Exceptional): Flawless structure, <1% filler rate, 120-150 WPM, and 100% factual accuracy.
 548 - 7 to 8 (Competent): Clear, direct answers with minor imperfections (1-2 fillers).
 549 - 5 to 6 (Average / Mediocre): Noticeable fillers ("um", "uh", "like"), surface-level explanations, or suboptimal WPM.
 550 - 3 to 4 (Weak): Frequent rambling, >4 fillers, hesitation pauses, or evasive responses.
 551 - 1 to 2 (Poor): Incoherent, factually wrong, or <10 words spoken.
 552 
 553 For `awkwardness` (1 to 10 scale, lower is better):
 554 - 1 = Composed and natural.
 555 - 4 to 6 = Noticeable filler words or hesitation pauses.
 556 - 7 to 10 = Severe awkwardness, frequent stutters, or losing train of thought.
 557 
 558 For `persuasive_assertiveness` (1 to 10 scale, higher indicates greater rhetorical conviction):
 559 - 9 to 10 = Unshakable conviction, authoritative delivery, decisive call-to-action, compelling impact.
 560 - 7 to 8 = Confident proposition, firm position, constructive advocacy.
 561 - 5 to 6 = Balanced or neutral stance, receptive to input, moderate conviction.
 562 - 3 to 4 = Tentative, overly cautious, yielding easily, passive posture.
 563 - 1 to 2 = Submissive, insecure, or unable to present a position.
 564 
 565 For `tone_style` (Categorical String describing the pragmatic intent & delivery posture):
 566 - Must be strictly one of: "Commanding" (authoritative, executive, directing), "Requesting" (soliciting input, seeking buy-in, asking questions), "Polite" (diplomatic, respectful, courteous), or "Collaborative" (partnership, shared-problem solving, team-oriented).
 567 
 568 === TASK 1: SCORE EVERY ACTIVE SPEAKER IN {json.dumps(speaker_roster)} ===
 569 Provide integer scores (1-10) for `communication_skills`, `fluency`, `confidence`, `awkwardness`, `straightforward_answers`, `persuasive_assertiveness`, a categorical string for `tone_style` ("Commanding", "Requesting", "Polite", or "Collaborative"), and a critical `rationale` citing exact quotes, WPM, and filler count.
 570 
 571 === TASK 2: VERBATIM Q&A FACT-CHECK AUDIT ===
 572 Extract every question asked and audit the response given. Assign a verdict strictly from: "Accurate", "Incomplete", or "Evasive".
 573 
 574 === FULL VERBATIM TRANSCRIPT ===
 575 {raw_transcript}
 576 
 577 Return ONLY valid JSON matching this schema:
 578 {{
 579   "speakerRatings": {{
 580     "<SpeakerName>": {{
 581       "communication_skills": <int 1-10>,
 582       "fluency": <int 1-10>,
 583       "confidence": <int 1-10>,
 584       "awkwardness": <int 1-10>,
 585       "straightforward_answers": <int 1-10>,
 586       "persuasive_assertiveness": <int 1-10>,
 587       "tone_style": "<Commanding | Requesting | Polite | Collaborative>",
 588       "rationale": "<critical evidence with verbatim quotes, WPM, and fillers>"
 589     }}
 590   }},
 591   "factCheckAudit": [
 592     {{
 593       "question_by": "<SpeakerName>",
 594       "question": "<Exact question>",
 595       "answered_by": "<SpeakerName>",
 596       "answer": "<Exact answer>",
 597       "verdict": "Accurate"
 598     }}
 599   ],
 600   "summary": "<2-3 sentence executive summary>"
 601 }}"""
 602 
 603         payload = {
 604             "contents": [{"parts": [{"text": prompt}]}],
 605             "generationConfig": {
 606                 "temperature": 0.1,
 607                 "responseMimeType": "application/json"
 608             }
 609         }
 610 
 611         models_to_try = ("gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash", "gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-2.0-flash", "gemini-1.5-flash")
 612         for key_candidate in keys_to_try:
 613             for model_id in models_to_try:
 614                 try:
 615                     url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model_id}:generateContent?key={key_candidate}"
 616                     async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:
 617                         resp = await client.post(url, json=payload)
 618                         if resp.status_code == 200:
 619                             candidates = resp.json().get("candidates", [])
 620                             if candidates:
 621                                 text_content = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [{}])[0].get("text", "")
 622                                 parsed = _parse_robust_json(text_content)
 623                                 if parsed and isinstance(parsed, dict):
 624                                     return _validate_and_clamp_analysis(
 625                                         parsed, speaker_roster, model_id, linguistic_metrics, analysis_segments, raw_transcript
 626                                     )
 627                         else:
 628                             print(f"[Gemini Scoring {model_id} Error] HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:250]}")
 629                 except Exception as e:
 630                     print(f"[Gemini Scoring {model_id} Exception] {e}")
 631 
 632     return _grounded_deterministic_analysis(analysis_segments, speaker_roster, linguistic_metrics)
 633 
 634 
 635 def _validate_and_clamp_analysis(
 636     parsed: Dict,
 637     speaker_roster: List[str],
 638     model_name: str,
 639     linguistic_metrics: Dict[str, Dict],
 640     dialogue_turns: List[Dict],
 641     rebuilt_transcript: str
 642 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 643     clean_host = speaker_roster[0]
 644 
 645     def clamp(v, default_val=5):
 646         try:
 647             return max(1, min(10, int(round(float(v)))))
 648         except (ValueError, TypeError):
 649             return default_val
 650 
 651     ratings_source = parsed.get("speakerRatings", parsed)
 652 
 653     def evaluate_guardrail(raw_data: Dict, spk_ling: Dict, spk_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 654         chars = spk_ling.get("chars", 0)
 655         words = spk_ling.get("words", 0)
 656         segs = spk_ling.get("segments", 0)
 657         fillers = spk_ling.get("fillers", 0)
 658         pauses = spk_ling.get("long_pauses", 0)
 659         wpm = spk_ling.get("wpm", 0)
 660         filler_rate = spk_ling.get("filler_rate_pct", 0.0)
 661 
 662         if chars < 15 or words < 3:
 663             return {
 664                 "communication_skills": 1, "fluency": 1, "confidence": 1, "awkwardness": 1, "straightforward_answers": 1,
 665                 "persuasive_assertiveness": 1, "tone_style": "Polite",
 666                 "rationale": "Insufficient spoken text (<3 words) for evaluation.",
 667                 "status_note": "Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.",
 668                 "is_clamped": True, "word_count": words, "char_count": chars, "segment_count": segs, "wpm": wpm, "filler_count": fillers
 669             }
 670 
 671         comm = clamp(raw_data.get("communication_skills"), 5)
 672         flu = clamp(raw_data.get("fluency"), 5)
 673         conf = clamp(raw_data.get("confidence"), 5)
 674         raw_awk = clamp(raw_data.get("awkwardness"), 3)
 675         straight = clamp(raw_data.get("straightforward_answers"), 5)
 676         persuasive = clamp(raw_data.get("persuasive_assertiveness"), conf)
 677 
 678         raw_tone = str(raw_data.get("tone_style") or "").strip().lower()
 679         if "command" in raw_tone:
 680             tone_style = "Commanding"
 681         elif "request" in raw_tone or "inquir" in raw_tone:
 682             tone_style = "Requesting"
 683         elif "polit" in raw_tone or "defer" in raw_tone:
 684             tone_style = "Polite"
 685         elif "collab" in raw_tone or "partner" in raw_tone:
 686             tone_style = "Collaborative"
 687         else:
 688             # Deterministic tone inference from assertiveness and speech
 689             if persuasive >= 8 or conf >= 8:
 690                 tone_style = "Commanding"
 691             elif "?" in raw_data.get("rationale", "") or "?" in str(raw_data):
 692                 tone_style = "Requesting"
 693             elif conf <= 4:
 694                 tone_style = "Polite"
 695             else:
 696                 tone_style = "Collaborative"
 697 
 698         if filler_rate > 6.0 or fillers >= 4:
 699             flu = min(flu, 5)
 700             comm = min(comm, 6)
 701             raw_awk = max(raw_awk, 6)
 702             persuasive = min(persuasive, 5)
 703         elif filler_rate > 3.0 or fillers >= 2:
 704             flu = min(flu, 6)
 705             raw_awk = max(raw_awk, 4)
 706 
 707         if pauses >= 2:
 708             flu = max(1, flu - 1)
 709             conf = max(1, conf - 1)
 710             persuasive = max(1, persuasive - 1)
 711             raw_awk = max(raw_awk, 5)
 712 
 713         if wpm > 0 and (wpm < 95 or wpm > 170):
 714             flu = max(1, flu - 1)
 715 
 716         if words < 18:
 717             comm = min(comm, 6)
 718             flu = min(flu, 6)
 719             conf = min(conf, 6)
 720             straight = min(straight, 6)
 721             persuasive = min(persuasive, 6)
 722 
 723         awk = raw_awk if (fillers > 0 or pauses > 0) else min(3, raw_awk)
 724 
 725         return {
 726             "communication_skills": comm,
 727             "fluency": flu,
 728             "confidence": conf,
 729             "awkwardness": awk,
 730             "straightforward_answers": straight,
 731             "persuasive_assertiveness": persuasive,
 732             "tone_style": tone_style,
 733             "rationale": raw_data.get("rationale") or f"Evaluated by Gemini 2.5 Flash at {wpm} WPM across {words} words ({segs} turns) with {fillers} fillers ({filler_rate}%). Tone posture: {tone_style}.",
 734             "status_note": f"Scored by {model_name} ({wpm} WPM, {fillers} fillers)",
 735             "is_clamped": False,
 736             "word_count": words,
 737             "char_count": chars,
 738             "segment_count": segs,
 739             "wpm": wpm,
 740             "filler_count": fillers
 741         }
 742 
 743     speaker_ratings_dict: Dict[str, Any] = {}
 744     all_detected_speakers = list(speaker_roster)
 745     for k in linguistic_metrics.keys():
 746         if k not in all_detected_speakers:
 747             all_detected_speakers.append(k)
 748 
 749     for idx, spk_name in enumerate(all_detected_speakers[:5]):
 750         spk_ling = linguistic_metrics.get(spk_name, {"words": 0, "chars": 0, "segments": 0, "fillers": 0, "wpm": 0, "long_pauses": 0, "filler_rate_pct": 0.0})
 751         if idx == 0 or spk_ling.get("words", 0) >= 3:
 752             raw_spk_data = ratings_source.get(spk_name, {})
 753             if not raw_spk_data:
 754                 for k, v in ratings_source.items():
 755                     if isinstance(v, dict) and (k.lower() == spk_name.lower() or spk_name.lower() in k.lower()):
 756                         raw_spk_data = v
 757                         break
 758             speaker_ratings_dict[spk_name] = evaluate_guardrail(raw_spk_data, spk_ling, spk_name)
 759 
 760     fact_check = []
 761     for item in parsed.get("factCheckAudit", []):
 762         if not isinstance(item, dict):
 763             continue
 764         q = (item.get("question") or "").strip()
 765         a = (item.get("answer") or "").strip()
 766         if not q or not a:
 767             continue
 768         v_raw = str(item.get("verdict", "")).lower()
 769         verdict = "Incomplete" if ("incom" in v_raw or "inacc" in v_raw) else ("Evasive" if "evas" in v_raw else "Accurate")
 770         fact_check.append({
 771             "question_by": item.get("question_by") or clean_host,
 772             "question": q,
 773             "answered_by": item.get("answered_by") or (speaker_roster[1] if len(speaker_roster) > 1 else clean_host),
 774             "answer": a,
 775             "verdict": verdict
 776         })
 777 
 778     return {
 779         "speakerRatings": speaker_ratings_dict,
 780         "factCheckAudit": fact_check,
 781         "dialogueTurns": dialogue_turns,
 782         "rawTranscript": rebuilt_transcript,
 783         "summary": parsed.get("summary") or f"Meeting for {', '.join(speaker_ratings_dict.keys())} diarized, transcribed, and scored by {model_name}.",
 784         "gemini_model": model_name
 785     }
 786 
 787 
 788 def _grounded_deterministic_analysis(
 789     dialogue_turns: List[Dict],
 790     speaker_roster: List[str],
 791     linguistic_metrics: Dict[str, Dict]
 792 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 793     clean_host = speaker_roster[0]
 794     questions_found = []
 795     for i, turn in enumerate(dialogue_turns):
 796         text = (turn.get("text") or "").strip()
 797         if "?" in text:
 798             answer_turn = dialogue_turns[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(dialogue_turns) else None
 799             if answer_turn and answer_turn.get("text"):
 800                 a_text = answer_turn["text"].strip()
 801                 verdict = "Incomplete" if len(a_text.split()) < 5 else "Accurate"
 802                 questions_found.append({
 803                     "question_by": turn.get("speaker", clean_host),
 804                     "question": text,
 805                     "answered_by": answer_turn.get("speaker", speaker_roster[-1]),
 806                     "answer": a_text,
 807                     "verdict": verdict
 808                 })
 809 
 810     speaker_ratings = {}
 811     for idx, spk_name in enumerate(speaker_roster):
 812         spk_ling = linguistic_metrics.get(spk_name, {"words": 0, "chars": 0, "segments": 0, "fillers": 0, "wpm": 0, "long_pauses": 0, "filler_rate_pct": 0.0})
 813         words = spk_ling.get("words", 0)
 814         chars = spk_ling.get("chars", 0)
 815         if idx == 0 or words >= 3:
 816             if words < 3:
 817                 speaker_ratings[spk_name] = {
 818                     "communication_skills": 1, "fluency": 1, "confidence": 1, "awkwardness": 1, "straightforward_answers": 1,
 819                     "persuasive_assertiveness": 1, "tone_style": "Polite",
 820                     "rationale": "Insufficient spoken text.", "status_note": "Insufficient spoken text",
 821                     "is_clamped": True, "word_count": words, "char_count": chars, "segment_count": 0, "wpm": 0, "filler_count": 0
 822                 }
 823             else:
 824                 is_host = (idx == 0)
 825                 tone = "Commanding" if is_host else "Collaborative"
 826                 speaker_ratings[spk_name] = {
 827                     "communication_skills": 6, "fluency": 6, "confidence": 6, "awkwardness": 3, "straightforward_answers": 6,
 828                     "persuasive_assertiveness": 6, "tone_style": tone,
 829                     "rationale": f"Spoke {words} words at {spk_ling.get('wpm', 130)} WPM.",
 830                     "status_note": "Evaluated via deterministic metrics",
 831                     "is_clamped": False, "word_count": words, "char_count": chars,
 832                     "segment_count": spk_ling.get("segments", 1), "wpm": spk_ling.get("wpm", 130), "filler_count": spk_ling.get("fillers", 0)
 833                 }
 834 
 835     rebuilt_transcript = "\n".join(f"{t.get('speaker', clean_host)}: {t.get('text', '')}" for t in dialogue_turns if t.get("text"))
 836     return {
 837         "speakerRatings": speaker_ratings,
 838         "factCheckAudit": questions_found,
 839         "dialogueTurns": dialogue_turns,
 840         "rawTranscript": rebuilt_transcript,
 841         "summary": f"Meeting analyzed for {', '.join(speaker_ratings.keys())}.",
 842         "gemini_model": "gemini-2.5-flash"
 843     }

Acoustic audio extraction, prosody & ffmpeg preprocessing

  14 # ----------------------------------------------------
  15 # Dynamic FFmpeg Resolution (Absolute Linux/Render/macOS Path)
  16 # ----------------------------------------------------
  17 def get_ffmpeg_binary() -> str:
  18     """
  19     Resolves the system's ffmpeg executable using absolute paths or PATH lookup.
  20     Prioritizes standard Linux locations used by Render's Debian/Ubuntu containers.
  21     """
  22     # 1. Custom environment variable override
  23     env_ffmpeg = os.environ.get("FFMPEG_PATH") or os.environ.get("FFMPEG_BIN")
  24     if env_ffmpeg and os.path.exists(env_ffmpeg):
  25         return env_ffmpeg
  26 
  27     # 2. Lookup via system PATH (e.g., /usr/bin/ffmpeg)
  28     which_ffmpeg = shutil.which("ffmpeg")
  29     if which_ffmpeg and os.path.exists(which_ffmpeg):
  30         return which_ffmpeg
  31 
  32     # 3. Known system locations (Linux Render container vs macOS Homebrew)
  33     candidates = [
  34         "/usr/bin/ffmpeg",             # Default Debian/Ubuntu apt install location
  35         "/usr/local/bin/ffmpeg",       # Common compiled/custom location
  36         "/bin/ffmpeg",                 # Core binary path
  37         "/opt/homebrew/bin/ffmpeg",    # macOS Apple Silicon
  38         "/usr/local/opt/ffmpeg/bin/ffmpeg" # macOS Intel
  39     ]
  40     for path in candidates:
  41         if os.path.exists(path) and os.access(path, os.X_OK):
  42             return path
  43 
  44     # 4. Fallback to command name for smooth PATH execution
  45     return which_ffmpeg or "ffmpeg"
  46 
  47 FFMPEG_BIN = get_ffmpeg_binary()
  48 
  49 # ----------------------------------------------------
  50 # Standard Temporary Storage (/tmp for Render container safety)
  51 # ----------------------------------------------------
  52 SYSTEM_TEMP_DIR = os.path.abspath(tempfile.gettempdir())
  53 UPLOAD_DIR = os.path.join(SYSTEM_TEMP_DIR, "guardianeye_uploads")
  54 AUDIO_OUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(SYSTEM_TEMP_DIR, "guardianeye_audio")
  55 
  56 os.makedirs(UPLOAD_DIR, exist_ok=True)
  57 os.makedirs(AUDIO_OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

  59 def format_timestamp(seconds: float) -> str:
  60     """Format seconds into MM:SS format."""
  61     mins = int(seconds // 60)
  62     secs = int(seconds % 60)
  63     return f"{mins:02d}:{secs:02d}"

  65 def extract_master_wav_from_video(video_path: str, output_wav_path: str) -> bool:
  66     """
  67     Extracts a 16kHz, 16-bit mono PCM WAV audio track from the video file using FFmpeg.
  68     Enforces absolute path tracking, /tmp temporary file guarantees, and smooth subprocess execution.
  69     """
  70     abs_video = os.path.abspath(video_path)
  71     abs_output = os.path.abspath(output_wav_path)
  72     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(abs_output), exist_ok=True)
  73 
  74     if not os.path.exists(abs_video) or os.path.getsize(abs_video) == 0:
  75         raise RuntimeError(f"Input video file is missing or empty at {abs_video}")
  76 
  77     ffmpeg_bin = get_ffmpeg_binary()
  78     print(f"[audio_extractor] Invoking FFmpeg: {ffmpeg_bin} -> {abs_output} (from {abs_video}, {os.path.getsize(abs_video)} bytes)")
  79 
  80     cmd = [
  81         ffmpeg_bin,
  82         "-y",                   # Overwrite output
  83         "-i", abs_video,        # Input video
  84         "-vn",                  # Disable video recording
  85         "-acodec", "pcm_s16le", # Uncompressed 16-bit PCM
  86         "-ar", "16000",         # 16,000 Hz sample rate
  87         "-ac", "1",             # Mono audio
  88         abs_output
  89     ]
  90 
  91     try:
  92         result = subprocess.run(
  93             cmd,
  94             stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
  95             stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  96             timeout=120
  97         )
  98     except FileNotFoundError:
  99         print(f"[audio_extractor] Binary {ffmpeg_bin} not found, falling back to 'ffmpeg' via PATH")
 100         cmd[0] = "ffmpeg"
 101         result = subprocess.run(
 102             cmd,
 103             stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
 104             stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
 105             timeout=120
 106         )
 107     except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 108         raise RuntimeError("FFmpeg extraction timed out after 120 seconds.")
 109 
 110     if result.returncode != 0:
 111         error_msg = result.stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')
 112         if "does not contain any stream" in error_msg or "matches no streams" in error_msg or "Invalid data found" in error_msg:
 113             print(f"[audio_extractor] Notice: No audio stream found in video ({error_msg[:100]}). Generating silent 3s WAV.")
 114             with wave.open(abs_output, "wb") as wf:
 115                 wf.setnchannels(1)
 116                 wf.setsampwidth(2)
 117                 wf.setframerate(16000)
 118                 wf.writeframes(b"\x00" * 32000 * 3)
 119         else:
 120             raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg audio extraction failed (code {result.returncode}): {error_msg}")
 121 
 122     if not os.path.exists(abs_output) or os.path.getsize(abs_output) == 0:
 123         raise RuntimeError(f"Extracted audio file is missing or 0 bytes at {abs_output}")
 124 
 125     return True
 126 
 127 def load_wav_samples(wav_path: str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
 128     """Reads uncompressed WAV file into normalized float32 numpy array and sample rate."""
 129     with wave.open(wav_path, "rb") as wf:
 130         n_channels = wf.getnchannels()
 131         sampwidth = wf.getsampwidth()
 132         framerate = wf.getframerate()
 133         n_frames = wf.getnframes()
 134         raw_bytes = wf.readframes(n_frames)
 135 
 136     if sampwidth == 2:
 137         samples = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=np.int16).astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
 138     elif sampwidth == 4:
 139         samples = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=np.int32).astype(np.float32) / 2147483648.0
 140     else:
 141         samples = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.float32) / 128.0 - 1.0
 142 
 143     return samples, framerate
 144 
 145 def detect_speech_segments(
 146     samples: np.ndarray,
 147     sr: int,
 148     frame_duration: float = 0.05,
 149     energy_threshold_percentile: float = 25.0,
 150     min_speech_duration: float = 0.8,
 151     min_silence_duration: float = 0.6
 152 ) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
 153     """
 154     Voice Activity Detection (VAD) via short-time root-mean-square energy (RMS).
 155     Identifies clean speech bursts and filters out background silence.
 156     """
 157     frame_size = int(sr * frame_duration)
 158     hop_size = frame_size // 2
 159     n_frames = (len(samples) - frame_size) // hop_size
 160 
 161     if n_frames <= 0:
 162         total_sec = len(samples) / sr
 163         return [(0.0, total_sec)] if total_sec > 0.5 else []
 164 
 165     # Calculate frame energies
 166     energies = np.zeros(n_frames)
 167     for i in range(n_frames):
 168         start_idx = i * hop_size
 169         frame = samples[start_idx : start_idx + frame_size]
 170         energies[i] = np.sqrt(np.mean(frame**2) + 1e-12)
 171 
 172     # Adaptive threshold
 173     mean_energy = np.mean(energies)
 174     noise_floor = np.percentile(energies, energy_threshold_percentile)
 175     threshold = noise_floor + 0.15 * (mean_energy - noise_floor)
 176     threshold = max(threshold, 0.012) # Floor guard
 177 
 178     # Speech vs silence mask
 179     is_speech = energies > threshold
 180 
 181     # Group into continuous intervals
 182     segments: List[Tuple[float, float]] = []
 183     in_speech = False
 184     speech_start_sec = 0.0
 185     silence_count = 0
 186     silence_frames_threshold = int(min_silence_duration / (hop_size / sr))
 187 
 188     for i, active in enumerate(is_speech):
 189         time_sec = (i * hop_size) / sr
 190         if active:
 191             if not in_speech:
 192                 in_speech = True
 193                 speech_start_sec = time_sec
 194             silence_count = 0
 195         else:
 196             if in_speech:
 197                 silence_count += 1
 198                 if silence_count >= silence_frames_threshold:
 199                     speech_end_sec = (i - silence_count) * hop_size / sr
 200                     if (speech_end_sec - speech_start_sec) >= min_speech_duration:
 201                         segments.append((round(speech_start_sec, 2), round(speech_end_sec, 2)))
 202                     in_speech = False
 203                     silence_count = 0
 204 
 205     if in_speech:
 206         speech_end_sec = len(samples) / sr
 207         if (speech_end_sec - speech_start_sec) >= min_speech_duration:
 208             segments.append((round(speech_start_sec, 2), round(speech_end_sec, 2)))
 209 
 210     # Fallback if no clean segments found
 211     if not segments and (len(samples) / sr) > 0.5:
 212         segments = [(0.0, round(len(samples) / sr, 2))]
 213 
 214     return segments
 215 
 216 def extract_acoustic_embedding(samples: np.ndarray, sr: int, start_sec: float, end_sec: float) -> np.ndarray:
 217     """
 218     Extracts acoustic speaker voice fingerprint (F0 pitch, spectral centroid, spectral rolloff, bandwidth).
 219     This vector distinguishes individual speaker voices from one another.
 220     """
 221     s_idx = max(0, int(start_sec * sr))
 222     e_idx = min(len(samples), int(end_sec * sr))
 223     clip = samples[s_idx:e_idx]
 224 
 225     if len(clip) < sr * 0.2:
 226         return np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)
 227 
 228     # 1. Fundamental frequency (Pitch estimation via autocorrelation)
 229     corr = np.correlate(clip[: min(len(clip), sr * 2)], clip[: min(len(clip), sr * 2)], mode='full')
 230     corr = corr[len(corr)//2 :]
 231     # Search in human vocal range: 75 Hz to 450 Hz
 232     min_lag = int(sr / 450)
 233     max_lag = int(sr / 75)
 234     lag_range = corr[min_lag:max_lag]
 235     pitch = 180.0
 236     if len(lag_range) > 0 and np.max(lag_range) > 0:
 237         pitch = float(sr / (min_lag + np.argmax(lag_range)))
 238 
 239     # 2. FFT Spectral analysis with Hanning window to eliminate boundary transients
 240     fft_clip = clip[: min(len(clip), sr * 3)]
 241     window = np.hanning(len(fft_clip))
 242     fft_vals = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(fft_clip * window))
 243     freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(fft_clip), 1.0 / sr)
 244     sum_fft = np.sum(fft_vals) + 1e-12
 245 
 246     # Spectral centroid (vocal brightness)
 247     centroid = float(np.sum(freqs * fft_vals) / sum_fft)
 248 
 249     # Spectral spread / bandwidth
 250     bandwidth = float(np.sqrt(np.sum(((freqs - centroid)**2) * fft_vals) / sum_fft))
 251 
 252     # Spectral rolloff (85% energy frequency)
 253     cumsum = np.cumsum(fft_vals)
 254     rolloff_idx = np.where(cumsum >= 0.85 * sum_fft)[0]
 255     rolloff = float(freqs[rolloff_idx[0]]) if len(rolloff_idx) > 0 else 3000.0
 256 
 257     # Zero crossing rate (articulation roughness)
 258     zcr = float(np.mean(np.abs(np.diff(np.sign(clip)))) / 2.0)
 259 
 260     # Dynamic RMS energy
 261     rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(clip**2)))
 262 
 263     return np.array([pitch, centroid, bandwidth, rolloff, zcr * 1000.0, rms * 100.0], dtype=np.float32)
 264 
 265 def cluster_speakers(
 266     embeddings: List[np.ndarray],
 267     max_speakers: int = 5
 268 ) -> List[int]:
 269     """
 270     Groups speech segment embeddings into distinct speaker identities (Speaker 1, Speaker 2, etc.)
 271     Uses feature standardization and distance-based clustering.
 272     """
 273     n_samples = len(embeddings)
 274     if n_samples == 0:
 275         return []
 276     if n_samples == 1:
 277         return [0]
 278 
 279     X = np.array(embeddings)
 280     # Normalize features
 281     std = np.std(X, axis=0) + 1e-6
 282     mean = np.mean(X, axis=0)
 283     X_norm = (X - mean) / std
 284 
 285     # Weight pitch and spectral centroid higher for speaker separation
 286     weights = np.array([2.5, 2.0, 1.2, 1.0, 1.0, 0.8])
 287     X_norm = X_norm * weights
 288 
 289     # Determine optimal speaker count (1 to min(n_samples, max_speakers))
 290     cluster_centers = [X_norm[0]]
 291     labels = [0] * n_samples
 292 
 293     for i in range(1, n_samples):
 294         dists = [np.linalg.norm(X_norm[i] - center) for center in cluster_centers]
 295         min_dist = min(dists)
 296         closest_cluster = int(np.argmin(dists))
 297 
 298         # Distance threshold: if voice is distinct enough and we haven't reached max_speakers
 299         # 1.55 allows distinct natural human voices to separate cleanly without collapsing
 300         if min_dist > 1.55 and len(cluster_centers) < max_speakers:
 301             cluster_centers.append(X_norm[i])
 302             labels[i] = int(len(cluster_centers) - 1)
 303         else:
 304             labels[i] = closest_cluster
 305             # Move center slightly toward new sample
 306             cluster_centers[closest_cluster] = 0.85 * cluster_centers[closest_cluster] + 0.15 * X_norm[i]
 307 
 308     return labels
 309 
 310 def slice_wav_segment(src_wav: str, dest_wav: str, start_sec: float, end_sec: float):
 311     """Extracts a precise time slice from an uncompressed WAV file."""
 312     with wave.open(src_wav, "rb") as r:
 313         framerate = r.getframerate()
 314         n_channels = r.getnchannels()
 315         sampwidth = r.getsampwidth()
 316         
 317         start_frame = int(start_sec * framerate)
 318         num_frames = int((end_sec - start_sec) * framerate)
 319         
 320         r.setpos(start_frame)
 321         frames = r.readframes(num_frames)
 322 
 323     with wave.open(dest_wav, "wb") as w:
 324         w.setnchannels(n_channels)
 325         w.setsampwidth(sampwidth)
 326         w.setframerate(framerate)
 327         w.writeframes(frames)
 328 
 329 def create_speaker_consolidated_wav(src_wav: str, dest_wav: str, intervals: List[Tuple[float, float]]):
 330     """Combines all speech segments spoken by a specific speaker into a single continuous WAV."""
 331     if not intervals:
 332         return
 333     with wave.open(src_wav, "rb") as r:
 334         framerate = r.getframerate()
 335         n_channels = r.getnchannels()
 336         sampwidth = r.getsampwidth()
 337         total_frames = r.getnframes()
 338         
 339         combined_frames = bytearray()
 340         for start_sec, end_sec in intervals:
 341             start_f = min(total_frames, max(0, int(start_sec * framerate)))
 342             end_f = min(total_frames, max(0, int(end_sec * framerate)))
 343             if end_f > start_f:
 344                 r.setpos(start_f)
 345                 combined_frames.extend(r.readframes(end_f - start_f))
 346 
 347     if not combined_frames:
 348         combined_frames = bytearray(b"\x00" * 1600)
 349 
 350     with wave.open(dest_wav, "wb") as w:
 351         w.setnchannels(n_channels)
 352         w.setsampwidth(sampwidth)
 353         w.setframerate(framerate)
 354         w.writeframes(bytes(combined_frames))
 355 
 356 def get_gemini_api_keys() -> Tuple[str, str]:
 357     """Resolves primary and secondary GEMINI_API_KEY from environment with load-splitting support."""
 358     def _clean(k: Optional[str]) -> str:
 359         s = (k or "").strip()
 360         if s in (
 361             "AIzaSy_YOUR_FIRST_GEMINI_API_KEY_HERE",
 362             "AIzaSy_YOUR_SECOND_GEMINI_API_KEY_HERE",
 363             "YOUR_GEMINI_API_KEY_HERE",
 364             "YOUR_GROQ_API_KEY_HERE"
 365         ):
 366             return ""
 367         return s
 368 
 369     primary = _clean(os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY_PRIMARY")) or _clean(os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"))
 370     secondary = _clean(os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY_SECONDARY"))
 371 
 372     groq_val = _clean(os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY"))
 373     if groq_val and (groq_val.startswith("AIza") or groq_val.startswith("AQ.")):
 374         if not primary: primary = groq_val
 375         elif not secondary: secondary = groq_val
 376 
 377     if not secondary: secondary = primary
 378     if not primary: primary = secondary
 379 
 380     return primary, secondary
 381 
 382 def get_gemini_api_key(role: str = "primary") -> str:
 383     """Resolves single GEMINI_API_KEY based on role ('primary' or 'secondary')."""
 384     p, s = get_gemini_api_keys()
 385     return p if role == "primary" else s
 386 
 387 def _prepare_gemini_audio(wav_path: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
 388     """Compresses WAV to compact 16kHz mono 48kbps MP3 for fast lightweight inline_data."""
 389     mp3_path = wav_path.rsplit(".", 1)[0] + "_gemini.mp3"
 390     ffmpeg_bin = get_ffmpeg_binary()
 391     cmd = [
 392         ffmpeg_bin, "-y",
 393         "-i", wav_path,
 394         "-vn",
 395         "-ar", "16000",
 396         "-ac", "1",
 397         "-b:a", "48k",
 398         mp3_path
 399     ]
 400     try:
 401         res = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=120)
 402         if res.returncode == 0 and os.path.exists(mp3_path) and os.path.getsize(mp3_path) > 100:
 403             return mp3_path, "audio/mp3"
 404     except Exception as e:
 405         print(f"[audio_extractor] MP3 compression notice: {e}")
 406     return wav_path, "audio/wav"
 407 
 408 def transcribe_master_with_gemini(wav_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
 409     """
 410     Rapidly transcribes the master WAV file using Google Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Multimodal Audio (inline_data).
 411     Zero local CPU/RAM overhead; prevents Render 512MB free tier OOM crashes and removes Groq API dependencies.
 412     """
 413     primary_key, secondary_key = get_gemini_api_keys()
 414     keys_to_try = [primary_key]
 415     if secondary_key and secondary_key != primary_key:
 416         keys_to_try.append(secondary_key)
 417 
 418     if not keys_to_try or not os.path.exists(wav_path) or os.path.getsize(wav_path) < 1000:
 419         return []
 420 
 421     upload_path, mime_type = _prepare_gemini_audio(wav_path)
 422     try:
 423         import base64
 424         import json
 425         import re
 426         import httpx
 427 
 428         with open(upload_path, "rb") as f:
 429             audio_b64 = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode("utf-8")
 430 
 431         prompt = """You are an expert audio transcription engine for 'Guardian Eye AI'.
 432 Transcribe the attached meeting/interview audio recording verbatim into chronological segments with accurate start and end timestamps in seconds.
 433 Preserve all natural speech and filler words ("um", "uh", "like", "you know") for accurate fluency auditing.
 434 
 435 Return ONLY valid JSON matching this exact schema:
 436 {
 437   "segments": [
 438     {
 439       "start": 0.0,
 440       "end": 3.5,
 441       "text": "Verbatim words spoken in this segment."
 442     }
 443   ]
 444 }"""
 445 
 446         payload = {
 447             "contents": [{
 448                 "parts": [
 449                     {"inline_data": {"mime_type": mime_type, "data": audio_b64}},
 450                     {"text": prompt}
 451                 ]
 452             }],
 453             "generationConfig": {
 454                 "temperature": 0.0,
 455                 "responseMimeType": "application/json"
 456             }
 457         }
 458 
 459         # Cascade through active Gemini Flash models
 460         models_to_try = (
 461             "gemini-2.5-flash",
 462             "gemini-3.5-flash",
 463             "gemini-3.8-flash",
 464             "gemini-flash-latest",
 465             "gemini-2.0-flash",
 466             "gemini-1.5-flash"
 467         )
 468 
 469         with httpx.Client(timeout=120.0) as client:
 470             for key_candidate in keys_to_try:
 471                 for model_id in models_to_try:
 472                     try:
 473                         url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model_id}:generateContent?key={key_candidate}"
 474                         resp = client.post(url, json=payload)
 475                         if resp.status_code == 200:
 476                             resp_json = resp.json()
 477                             candidates = resp_json.get("candidates", [])
 478                             if candidates:
 479                                 text_content = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [{}])[0].get("text", "")
 480                                 clean_json = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", text_content.strip(), flags=re.MULTILINE)
 481                                 clean_json = re.sub(r"```$", "", clean_json.strip(), flags=re.MULTILINE)
 482                                 parsed = json.loads(clean_json)
 483                                 segs = parsed.get("segments", []) if isinstance(parsed, dict) else (parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else [])
 484                                 if segs:
 485                                     print(f"[audio_extractor] Gemini ({model_id}) transcribed master audio into {len(segs)} segments.")
 486                                     return segs
 487                         else:
 488                             print(f"[audio_extractor] Gemini {model_id} returned HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:120]}")
 489                     except Exception as m_err:
 490                         print(f"[audio_extractor] Gemini {model_id} notice: {m_err}")
 491     except Exception as e:
 492         print(f"[audio_extractor] Gemini transcription exception: {e}")
 493     finally:
 494         if upload_path != wav_path and os.path.exists(upload_path):
 495             try:
 496                 os.remove(upload_path)
 497             except Exception:
 498                 pass
 499     return []
 500 
 501 # Backward-compatibility alias
 502 transcribe_master_with_groq = transcribe_master_with_gemini

 504 def process_video_diarization(
 505     video_path: str,
 506     video_id: str,
 507     host_name: Optional[str] = None,
 508     participant_names: Optional[List[str]] = None
 509 ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 510     """
 511     Main Pipeline:
 512     1. Extracts master 16kHz WAV from video via FFmpeg in /tmp.
 513     2. Runs Voice Activity Detection to find who spoke when.
 514     3. Extracts voice embeddings and clusters by speaker.
 515     4. Slices individual audio clips for every speaker turn.
 516     5. Combines per-speaker speech into consolidated speaker WAV files.
 517     6. Returns complete timeline with timestamps and audio stream URLs.
 518     """
 519     session_audio_dir = os.path.join(AUDIO_OUTPUT_DIR, video_id)
 520     os.makedirs(session_audio_dir, exist_ok=True)
 521 
 522     master_wav_path = os.path.join(session_audio_dir, "master_audio.wav")
 523     extract_master_wav_from_video(video_path, master_wav_path)
 524 
 525     samples, sr = load_wav_samples(master_wav_path)
 526     total_duration_sec = len(samples) / sr
 527 
 528     # Step 1: Detect speech intervals
 529     speech_segments = detect_speech_segments(samples, sr)
 530     if not speech_segments:
 531         total_sec = max(round(total_duration_sec, 2), 1.0)
 532         speech_segments = [(0.0, total_sec)]
 533 
 534     # Step 2: Extract acoustic voice embeddings
 535     embeddings = [
 536         extract_acoustic_embedding(samples, sr, start, end)
 537         for (start, end) in speech_segments
 538     ]
 539 
 540     # Step 3: Cluster into speaker labels (Speaker 0, 1, ...)
 541     speaker_labels = cluster_speakers(embeddings, max_speakers=5)
 542     if not speaker_labels:
 543         speaker_labels = [0] * len(speech_segments)
 544 
 545     # Dynamic speaker names matching meeting roles (Host vs Participants)
 546     clean_host = (host_name or "").strip()
 547     if clean_host:
 548         host_display = clean_host if clean_host.lower().endswith("(host)") else f"{clean_host} (Host)"
 549     else:
 550         host_display = "Host"
 551 
 552     names_pool = [host_display]
 553     if participant_names:
 554         for p in participant_names:
 555             if p and p.strip():
 556                 clean_p = p.strip()
 557                 if not clean_p.lower().endswith("(participant)") and not clean_p.lower().endswith("(host)"):
 558                     names_pool.append(f"{clean_p} (Participant)")
 559                 else:
 560                     names_pool.append(clean_p)
 561 
 562     # Fill remaining participant spots with descriptive labels
 563     for i in range(len(names_pool), 10):
 564         names_pool.append(f"Participant {i}")
 565 
 566     # Step 4: Rapid transcription pass (Groq Cloud if available)
 567     groq_segments = transcribe_master_with_groq(master_wav_path)
 568 
 569     # Step 5: Organize turns and slice audio files
 570     speakers_dict: Dict[int, dict] = {}
 571     chronological_timeline: List[dict] = []
 572     total_speech_time = 0.0
 573 
 574     for idx, ((start, end), spk_id) in enumerate(zip(speech_segments, speaker_labels)):
 575         duration = round(end - start, 2)
 576         total_speech_time += duration
 577 
 578         # Slice individual turn audio
 579         turn_filename = f"speaker_{spk_id + 1}_turn_{idx + 1}.wav"
 580         turn_path = os.path.join(session_audio_dir, turn_filename)
 581         slice_wav_segment(master_wav_path, turn_path, start, end)
 582         turn_url = f"/api/audio/stream/{video_id}/{turn_filename}"
 583 
 584         spk_name = names_pool[spk_id] if spk_id < len(names_pool) else f"Participant {spk_id}"
 585         spk_role = "Host" if spk_id == 0 else "Participant"
 586 
 587         # Transcribe turn: Priority 1 = Groq segments matching timestamp
 588         turn_text = ""
 589         if groq_segments:
 590             overlapping = [
 591                 seg.get("text", "").strip()
 592                 for seg in groq_segments
 593                 if max(start, float(seg.get("start", 0.0))) < min(end, float(seg.get("end", end)))
 594                 and seg.get("text", "").strip()
 595             ]
 596             if overlapping:
 597                 turn_text = " ".join(overlapping)
 598 
 599         # Priority 2: Local Whisper ONLY if explicitly enabled (to protect 512MB RAM from OOM)
 600         if not turn_text and os.environ.get("USE_LOCAL_WHISPER", "").lower() == "true":
 601             try:
 602                 from diarizer import get_whisper_model
 603                 model = get_whisper_model("tiny")
 604                 if model is not None and os.path.exists(turn_path) and os.path.getsize(turn_path) > 1000:
 605                     res = model.transcribe(turn_path, fp16=False, language="en")
 606                     turn_text = res.get("text", "").strip()
 607             except Exception:
 608                 pass
 609 
 610         if not turn_text:
 611             continue
 612 
 613         turn_item = {
 614             "turnIndex": idx + 1,
 615             "speakerId": f"SPEAKER_{spk_id + 1}",
 616             "speakerName": spk_name,
 617             "role": spk_role,
 618             "startTimeSec": start,
 619             "endTimeSec": end,
 620             "start": format_timestamp(start),
 621             "end": format_timestamp(end),
 622             "timestamp": f"{format_timestamp(start)} - {format_timestamp(end)}",
 623             "duration": duration,
 624             "durationFormatted": f"{round(duration, 1)}s",
 625             "text": turn_text,
 626             "audioUrl": turn_url
 627         }
 628         chronological_timeline.append(turn_item)
 629 
 630         if spk_id not in speakers_dict:
 631             speakers_dict[spk_id] = {
 632                 "id": f"SPEAKER_{spk_id + 1}",
 633                 "name": spk_name,
 634                 "role": spk_role,
 635                 "totalSpokenSec": 0.0,
 636                 "turnCount": 0,
 637                 "turns": []
 638             }
 639 
 640         speakers_dict[spk_id]["totalSpokenSec"] += duration
 641         speakers_dict[spk_id]["turnCount"] += 1
 642         speakers_dict[spk_id]["turns"].append(turn_item)
 643 
 644     # Generate combined audio track for each speaker
 645     speakers_list = []
 646     for spk_id, spk_data in sorted(speakers_dict.items()):
 647         spk_data["totalSpokenSec"] = round(spk_data["totalSpokenSec"], 2)
 648         percentage = round((spk_data["totalSpokenSec"] / max(total_duration_sec, 1.0)) * 100, 1)
 649         spk_data["speakingPercentage"] = percentage
 650         spk_data["formattedSpokenTime"] = format_timestamp(spk_data["totalSpokenSec"])
 651         
 652         # Build consolidated audio of all turns for this speaker
 653         speaker_intervals = [(t["startTimeSec"], t["endTimeSec"]) for t in spk_data["turns"]]
 654         spk_full_filename = f"speaker_{spk_id + 1}_full.wav"
 655         spk_full_path = os.path.join(session_audio_dir, spk_full_filename)
 656         create_speaker_consolidated_wav(master_wav_path, spk_full_path, speaker_intervals)
 657         spk_data["fullAudioUrl"] = f"/api/audio/stream/{video_id}/{spk_full_filename}"
 658         
 659         # Primary sample audio for this speaker
 660         if spk_data["turns"]:
 661             spk_data["sampleAudioUrl"] = spk_data["turns"][0]["audioUrl"]
 662         else:
 663             spk_data["sampleAudioUrl"] = spk_data["fullAudioUrl"]
 664 
 665         # Run unbiased behavioral & acoustic analysis algorithms
 666         is_host = (spk_data["role"] == "Host" or spk_id == 0)
 667         spk_analytics = analyze_speaker_acoustics(
 668             samples=samples,
 669             sr=sr,
 670             turns=spk_data["turns"],
 671             total_meeting_sec=total_duration_sec,
 672             total_meeting_turns=len(chronological_timeline),
 673             speaker_count=len(speakers_dict),
 674             is_host=is_host
 675         )
 676         spk_data["analytics"] = spk_analytics
 677         
 678         speakers_list.append(spk_data)
 679 
 680     silence_duration = max(0.0, round(total_duration_sec - total_speech_time, 2))
 681     meeting_insights = generate_meeting_insights(speakers_list, total_duration_sec)
 682 
 683     dialogue_script = "\n".join([f"{t['speakerName']}: {t['text']}" for t in chronological_timeline if t.get("text")])
 684 
 685     return {
 686         "videoId": video_id,
 687         "masterAudioUrl": f"/api/audio/stream/{video_id}/master_audio.wav",
 688         "totalDurationSec": round(total_duration_sec, 2),
 689         "totalDurationFormatted": format_timestamp(total_duration_sec),
 690         "totalSpeechSec": round(total_speech_time, 2),
 691         "silenceDurationSec": silence_duration,
 692         "silencePercentage": round((silence_duration / max(total_duration_sec, 1.0)) * 100, 1),
 693         "speakerCount": len(speakers_list),
 694         "speakers": speakers_list,
 695         "timeline": chronological_timeline,
 696         "meetingInsights": meeting_insights,
 697         "dialogueScript": dialogue_script
 698     }
PYTHON backend/communication_coach_prompt.py

Gemini AI system prompts & coaching heuristics
   1 """
   2 GuardianEye AI - Communication Skills Analysis Engine Prompt Module
   3 Provides production-grade prompt templating, scoring rubrics, and Pydantic validation
   4 for evaluating speaker communication behavior from timestamped transcripts and audio statistics.
   5 """
   6 
   7 import json
   8 from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any
   9 from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
  10 
  11 # ==============================================================================
  12 # 1. PYDANTIC SCHEMA DEFINITIONS
  13 # ==============================================================================
  14 
  15 class SpeechMetrics(BaseModel):
  16     speaking_duration_seconds: Optional[float] = Field(None, description="Total speaking time in seconds")
  17     word_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="Total words spoken")
  18     words_per_minute: Optional[float] = Field(None, description="Speaking rate in WPM")
  19     filler_word_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="Number of detected filler words")
  20     speaking_turn_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="Number of speaking turns")
  21 
  22 class CategoryScores(BaseModel):
  23     verbal_clarity: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Articulative clarity and phonetic precision (Weight: 20%)")
  24     speaking_fluency: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Cadence, pacing, and pause control (Weight: 15%)")
  25     vocabulary_and_expression: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Word precision and nuance (Weight: 15%)")
  26     explanation_and_presentation: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Logical progression and framing (Weight: 15%)")
  27     active_listening_and_responsiveness: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Direct answering and acknowledging peers (Weight: 15%)")
  28     professional_communication: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Diplomacy, constructive tone, respect (Weight: 10%)")
  29     conciseness_and_structure: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Brevity and bottom-line up front (Weight: 10%)")
  30     persuasive_assertiveness: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Rhetorical conviction, stance firmness & persuasive impact")
  31     tone_style: Optional[str] = Field("Collaborative", description="Pragmatic intent & delivery posture: Commanding, Requesting, Polite, or Collaborative")
  32 
  33 class StrengthItem(BaseModel):
  34     title: str = Field(..., description="Concise label of the communication strength")
  35     explanation: str = Field(..., description="Why this behavior was effective in the meeting context")
  36     evidence_quote: str = Field(..., description="Exact verbatim quotation from the transcript")
  37     timestamp: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Timestamp where this quotation occurred (e.g., '04:12')")
  38 
  39 class ImprovementAreaItem(BaseModel):
  40     title: str = Field(..., description="Actionable label of the area for improvement")
  41     explanation: str = Field(..., description="Specific pattern observed and its impact on listeners")
  42     evidence_quote: str = Field(..., description="Exact verbatim quotation demonstrating the pattern")
  43     timestamp: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Timestamp where this quotation occurred")
  44     suggested_improvement: str = Field(..., description="Concrete alternative phrasing or behavioral adjustment")
  45 
  46 class PracticePlanItem(BaseModel):
  47     skill: str = Field(..., description="The targeted communication skill")
  48     exercise: str = Field(..., description="Specific, practical exercise the speaker can perform")
  49     measurable_goal: str = Field(..., description="Concrete, trackable metric for improvement")
  50 
  51 class EvidenceCoverage(BaseModel):
  52     level: str = Field(..., description="Confidence level: 'high', 'medium', 'low', or 'insufficient'")
  53     limitations: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Known gaps or missing signals")
  54 
  55 class CommunicationCoachingReport(BaseModel):
  56     speaker_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Identifier of the evaluated speaker")
  57     assessment_type: str = Field("communication_coaching", description="Assessment type discriminator")
  58     metrics: SpeechMetrics
  59     category_scores: CategoryScores
  60     persuasive_assertiveness: Optional[float] = Field(None, description="Persuasive assertiveness (1-10 or 0-100)")
  61     tone_style: Optional[str] = Field("Collaborative", description="Delivery posture: Commanding, Requesting, Polite, Collaborative")
  62     overall_score: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="Renormalized weighted average")
  63     strengths: List[StrengthItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
  64     improvement_areas: List[ImprovementAreaItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
  65     practice_plan: List[PracticePlanItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
  66     evidence_coverage: EvidenceCoverage
  67     overall_feedback: str = Field(..., description="High-level executive summary and coaching recommendation")
  68 
  69 
  70 # ==============================================================================
  71 # 2. CATEGORY WEIGHTS & RENORMALIZATION HELPER
  72 # ==============================================================================
  73 
  74 CATEGORY_WEIGHTS: Dict[str, float] = {
  75     "verbal_clarity": 0.20,
  76     "speaking_fluency": 0.15,
  77     "vocabulary_and_expression": 0.15,
  78     "explanation_and_presentation": 0.15,
  79     "active_listening_and_responsiveness": 0.15,
  80     "professional_communication": 0.10,
  81     "conciseness_and_structure": 0.10,
  82 }
  83 
  84 def calculate_renormalized_overall_score(category_scores: Dict[str, Optional[float]]) -> Optional[float]:
  85     """
  86     Computes weighted score renormalized across only assessable (non-null) categories.
  87     Returns None if no categories are assessable.
  88     """
  89     total_weighted_points = 0.0
  90     total_active_weight = 0.0
  91 
  92     for cat, weight in CATEGORY_WEIGHTS.items():
  93         score = category_scores.get(cat)
  94         if score is not None:
  95             total_weighted_points += score * weight
  96             total_active_weight += weight
  97 
  98     if total_active_weight <= 0:
  99         return None
 100 
 101     return round(total_weighted_points / total_active_weight, 1)
 102
