JAVASCRIPT functions/index.js
1,696 lines • 65.6 KB
Firebase Cloud Functions (auth, meetings, analysis triggers)
   1 const { onCall, HttpsError } = require("firebase-functions/v2/https");
   2 const { defineSecret } = require("firebase-functions/params");
   3 const admin = require("firebase-admin");
   4 const fs = require("fs");
   5 const path = require("path");
   6 const os = require("os");
   7 const { execFile } = require("child_process");
   8 const { promisify } = require("util");
   9 const execFileAsync = promisify(execFile);
  10 
  11 if (!admin.apps.length) {
  12   admin.initializeApp();
  13 }
  14 
  15 const db = admin.firestore();
  16 const storage = admin.storage();
  17 
  18 const { GoogleGenerativeAI } = require("@google/generative-ai");
  19 
  20 // Securely store API Keys inside Google Cloud Secret Manager / Firebase Functions Config
  21 const deepgramApiKey = defineSecret("DEEPGRAM_API_KEY");
  22 const groqApiKey = defineSecret("GROQ_API_KEY");
  23 const geminiApiKey = defineSecret("GEMINI_API_KEY");
  24 
  25 /**
  26  * Safely resolves a secret parameter with environment variable fallback.
  27  * Prevents "Secret is not bound to this function" runtime crashes.
  28  */
  29 function resolveSecret(secretParam, envName) {
  30   if (secretParam) {
  31     try {
  32       const val = secretParam.value();
  33       if (val && typeof val === "string" && val.trim().length > 0) {
  34         return val.trim();
  35       }
  36     } catch (e) {
  37       // Secret is not bound in current execution context or emulator
  38     }
  39   }
  40   return (process.env[envName] || "").trim();
  41 }
  42 
  43 /**
  44  * Helper to format Deepgram Nova-2 diarized timeline payload
  45  * into chronological user-by-user dialogue text
  46  */
  47 function compileDiarizedDialogue(deepgramData, speakerMap) {
  48   const channel = deepgramData?.results?.channels?.[0];
  49   const alternative = channel?.alternatives?.[0];
  50 
  51   if (!alternative) {
  52     return { dialogueText: "", turns: [] };
  53   }
  54 
  55   const turns = [];
  56 
  57   // 1. Try extracting paragraphs if present
  58   const paragraphs = alternative.paragraphs?.paragraphs;
  59   if (Array.isArray(paragraphs) && paragraphs.length > 0) {
  60     for (const para of paragraphs) {
  61       const speakerIdx = typeof para.speaker === "number" ? para.speaker : 0;
  62       const speakerName = speakerMap[speakerIdx] || `Speaker ${speakerIdx}`;
  63       const text = para.sentences?.map((s) => s.text).join(" ") || "";
  64       if (text.trim()) {
  65         turns.push({
  66           speakerId: `SPEAKER_${speakerIdx}`,
  67           speaker: speakerName,
  68           isHost: speakerIdx === 0,
  69           role: speakerIdx === 0 ? "Host" : "User",
  70           text: text.trim(),
  71           start: para.start != null ? formatTime(para.start) : "",
  72           timestamp: para.start != null ? formatTime(para.start) : ""
  73         });
  74       }
  75     }
  76   } else if (Array.isArray(alternative.words) && alternative.words.length > 0) {
  77     // 2. Group sequential words with the same speaker ID
  78     let currentSpeaker = null;
  79     let currentWords = [];
  80     let turnStart = 0;
  81 
  82     for (const wordObj of alternative.words) {
  83       const spk = typeof wordObj.speaker === "number" ? wordObj.speaker : 0;
  84       if (currentSpeaker === null) {
  85         currentSpeaker = spk;
  86         turnStart = wordObj.start;
  87       }
  88 
  89       if (spk !== currentSpeaker && currentWords.length > 0) {
  90         const speakerName = speakerMap[currentSpeaker] || `Speaker ${currentSpeaker}`;
  91         turns.push({
  92           speakerId: `SPEAKER_${currentSpeaker}`,
  93           speaker: speakerName,
  94           isHost: currentSpeaker === 0,
  95           role: currentSpeaker === 0 ? "Host" : "User",
  96           text: currentWords.join(" ").trim(),
  97           start: formatTime(turnStart),
  98           timestamp: formatTime(turnStart)
  99         });
 100         currentSpeaker = spk;
 101         currentWords = [];
 102         turnStart = wordObj.start;
 103       }
 104 
 105       currentWords.push(wordObj.punctuated_word || wordObj.word);
 106     }
 107 
 108     if (currentWords.length > 0) {
 109       const speakerName = speakerMap[currentSpeaker] || `Speaker ${currentSpeaker}`;
 110       turns.push({
 111         speakerId: `SPEAKER_${currentSpeaker}`,
 112         speaker: speakerName,
 113         isHost: currentSpeaker === 0,
 114         role: currentSpeaker === 0 ? "Host" : "User",
 115         text: currentWords.join(" ").trim(),
 116         start: formatTime(turnStart),
 117         timestamp: formatTime(turnStart)
 118       });
 119     }
 120   } else if (alternative.transcript) {
 121     // Fallback if no diarization words returned
 122     const defaultSpeaker = speakerMap[0] || "Host";
 123     turns.push({
 124       speakerId: "SPEAKER_0",
 125       speaker: defaultSpeaker,
 126       isHost: true,
 127       role: "Host",
 128       text: alternative.transcript.trim(),
 129       start: "00:00",
 130       timestamp: "00:00"
 131     });
 132   }
 133 
 134   // Format clean dialogue text from speaker turns
 135   const dialogueText = turns.map((t) => `${t.speaker}: ${t.text}`).join("\n");
 136 
 137   return { dialogueText, turns };
 138 }
 139 
 140 function formatTime(seconds) {
 141   if (typeof seconds !== "number" || isNaN(seconds)) return "00:00";
 142   const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
 143   const secs = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
 144   return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
 145 }
 146 
 147 /**
 148  * HTTPS Callable Cloud Function: transcribeMeetingAudio
 149  * Triggers after video file is securely uploaded to Firebase Storage
 150  * Streams audio to Deepgram Nova-2 with diarize=true&smart_format=true&model=nova-2
 151  * Saves compiled dialogue into Firestore collection 'transcripts'
 152  */
 153 exports.transcribeMeetingAudio = onCall(
 154   {
 155     secrets: [deepgramApiKey],
 156     memory: "1GiB",
 157     timeoutSeconds: 300,
 158     cors: true
 159   },
 160   async (request) => {
 161     // 1. Authenticate user
 162     if (!request.auth) {
 163       throw new HttpsError(
 164         "unauthenticated",
 165         "Authentication required: only authenticated users can access meeting transcription."
 166       );
 167     }
 168 
 169     const userId = request.auth.uid;
 170     const {
 171       meetingId,
 172       storagePath,
 173       hostName: overrideHost,
 174       participantNames: overrideParticipants
 175     } = request.data || {};
 176 
 177     if (!meetingId) {
 178       throw new HttpsError("invalid-argument", "Missing meetingId parameter.");
 179     }
 180 
 181     const filePath = storagePath || `meetings/${userId}/${meetingId}.mp4`;
 182 
 183     console.log(`[transcribeMeetingAudio] Processing meeting ${meetingId} for user ${userId} at path ${filePath}`);
 184 
 185     // Update transcript status to 'processing'
 186     const transcriptRef = db.collection("transcripts").doc(meetingId);
 187     await transcriptRef.set(
 188       {
 189         meetingId,
 190         userId,
 191         status: "processing",
 192         updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 193       },
 194       { merge: true }
 195     );
 196 
 197     // 2. Fetch Meeting Metadata from Firestore (to pull authenticated participant names)
 198     let hostName = overrideHost;
 199     let guestName = Array.isArray(overrideParticipants) && overrideParticipants[0];
 200 
 201     try {
 202       const meetingSnap = await db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId).get();
 203       if (meetingSnap.exists) {
 204         const meetingData = meetingSnap.data();
 205         if (!hostName && meetingData.hostName) hostName = meetingData.hostName;
 206         if (!guestName) {
 207           if (meetingData.participants && meetingData.participants.length > 0) {
 208             guestName = meetingData.participants[0].name || meetingData.participants[0];
 209           } else if (meetingData.participantNames && meetingData.participantNames.length > 0) {
 210             guestName = meetingData.participantNames[0];
 211           }
 212         }
 213       }
 214     } catch (metaErr) {
 215       console.warn("[transcribeMeetingAudio] Could not fetch meeting doc metadata:", metaErr.message);
 216     }
 217 
 218     if (!hostName) hostName = "Host";
 219     if (!guestName) guestName = "Participant";
 220 
 221     // Build dynamic Speaker mapping: Speaker 0 -> Host, Speaker 1 -> Participant
 222     const speakerMap = {
 223       0: hostName,
 224       1: guestName
 225     };
 226 
 227     // 3. Download audio/video file from Firebase Storage
 228     const bucket = storage.bucket();
 229     const file = bucket.file(filePath);
 230 
 231     const [exists] = await file.exists();
 232     if (!exists) {
 233       // Check if file exists under recordings/ or with webm
 234       throw new HttpsError(
 235         "not-found",
 236         `Storage file not found at ${filePath}. Ensure file upload completed successfully.`
 237       );
 238     }
 239 
 240     const [fileBuffer] = await file.download();
 241 
 242     // 4. Stream directly to Deepgram Speech-to-Text API
 243     // Parameters: diarize=true&smart_format=true&model=nova-2
 244     const apiKey = resolveSecret(deepgramApiKey, "DEEPGRAM_API_KEY");
 245     if (!apiKey) {
 246       console.warn("[transcribeMeetingAudio] DEEPGRAM_API_KEY not configured. Returning empty evaluation matrix.");
 247       const payload = {
 248         meetingId,
 249         userId,
 250         transcriptText: "",
 251         status: "completed",
 252         speakers: [
 253           { id: 0, name: hostName, role: "host" }
 254         ],
 255         dialogueTurns: [],
 256         createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 257       };
 258       await transcriptRef.set(payload, { merge: true });
 259       return payload;
 260     }
 261 
 262     const deepgramUrl = "https://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?diarize=true&smart_format=true&model=nova-2";
 263 
 264     const deepgramResponse = await fetch(deepgramUrl, {
 265       method: "POST",
 266       headers: {
 267         Authorization: `Token ${apiKey}`,
 268         "Content-Type": "video/mp4"
 269       },
 270       body: fileBuffer
 271     });
 272 
 273     if (!deepgramResponse.ok) {
 274       const errBody = await deepgramResponse.text();
 275       console.error("[transcribeMeetingAudio] Deepgram API error:", deepgramResponse.status, errBody);
 276       throw new HttpsError(
 277         "internal",
 278         `Deepgram API error (${deepgramResponse.status}): ${errBody}`
 279       );
 280     }
 281 
 282     const deepgramResult = await deepgramResponse.json();
 283 
 284     // 5. Map Speaker 0 & 1 and format into chronological plain text dialogue
 285     const { dialogueText, turns } = compileDiarizedDialogue(deepgramResult, speakerMap);
 286 
 287     const transcriptPayload = {
 288       meetingId,
 289       userId,
 290       transcriptText: dialogueText,
 291       status: "completed",
 292       speakers: [
 293         { id: 0, name: hostName, role: "host" },
 294         { id: 1, name: guestName, role: "participant" }
 295       ],
 296       dialogueTurns: turns,
 297       createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 298     };
 299 
 300     // 6. Save compiled transcript inside Firestore 'transcripts' collection
 301     await transcriptRef.set(transcriptPayload, { merge: true });
 302 
 303     // Also update meeting status
 304     try {
 305       await db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId).set(
 306         {
 307           transcriptStatus: "completed",
 308           updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 309         },
 310         { merge: true }
 311       );
 312     } catch { }
 313 
 314     console.log(`[transcribeMeetingAudio] Successfully completed transcript for meeting ${meetingId}`);
 315 
 316     return {
 317       success: true,
 318       meetingId,
 319       transcriptText: dialogueText,
 320       status: "completed",
 321       dialogueTurns: turns
 322     };
 323   }
 324 );
 325 
 326 /**
 327  * Helper to dynamically resolve the ffmpeg binary path across macOS, Linux, and Cloud Functions
 328  */
 329 function resolveFfmpegPath() {
 330   const candidatePaths = [
 331     process.env.FFMPEG_PATH,
 332     "/opt/homebrew/bin/ffmpeg",
 333     "/usr/local/bin/ffmpeg",
 334     "/usr/bin/ffmpeg",
 335     "ffmpeg"
 336   ].filter(Boolean);
 337 
 338   for (const p of candidatePaths) {
 339     try {
 340       if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
 341         return p;
 342       }
 343     } catch { }
 344   }
 345   return "ffmpeg";
 346 }
 347 
 348 /**
 349  * Audio Track Extraction Helper
 350  * Extracts 16kHz mono PCM WAV Audio using ffmpeg for optimal Groq Whisper model accuracy
 351  */
 352 async function extractAudioTrack(videoBuffer, filenamePrefix = "meeting") {
 353   const tempDir = path.resolve(os.tmpdir());
 354   const inputPath = path.join(tempDir, `${filenamePrefix}_${Date.now()}_input.mp4`);
 355   const outputPath = path.join(tempDir, `${filenamePrefix}_${Date.now()}_audio_16k.wav`);
 356 
 357   if (!videoBuffer || videoBuffer.length === 0) {
 358     console.error("[extractAudioTrack] ERROR: Input video buffer is empty (0 bytes).");
 359     throw new Error("Input video buffer is empty (0 bytes). Cannot extract audio track.");
 360   }
 361 
 362   console.log(`[extractAudioTrack] Received video buffer (${videoBuffer.length} bytes). Writing to ${inputPath}...`);
 363   await fs.promises.writeFile(inputPath, videoBuffer);
 364   const ffmpegBinary = resolveFfmpegPath();
 365   console.log(`[extractAudioTrack] Using ffmpeg binary: ${ffmpegBinary}`);
 366 
 367   try {
 368     // Explicitly convert input video into 16kHz mono PCM WAV format (-acodec pcm_s16le -ar 16000 -ac 1)
 369     await execFileAsync(ffmpegBinary, [
 370       "-y",
 371       "-i", inputPath,
 372       "-vn",
 373       "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
 374       "-ar", "16000",
 375       "-ac", "1",
 376       outputPath
 377     ]);
 378 
 379     // Verify output file existence & non-zero size (> 0 bytes and >= 44 bytes header)
 380     if (!fs.existsSync(outputPath)) {
 381       throw new Error("FFmpeg failed to produce output WAV file.");
 382     }
 383 
 384     const stat = await fs.promises.stat(outputPath);
 385     if (stat.size === 0 || stat.size < 44) {
 386       throw new Error(`Output audio file size is ${stat.size} bytes (<44-byte WAV header or 0 bytes). Cannot pass to Groq Whisper Large-V3.`);
 387     }
 388 
 389     // Audio stream duration matching validation
 390     const videoDuration = await probeMediaDuration(inputPath, ffmpegBinary);
 391     const audioDuration = await probeMediaDuration(outputPath, ffmpegBinary);
 392     console.log(`[extractAudioTrack] Duration audit: Video=${videoDuration != null ? videoDuration.toFixed(2) + 's' : 'N/A'}, Audio=${audioDuration != null ? audioDuration.toFixed(2) + 's' : 'N/A'}`);
 393 
 394     if (videoDuration != null && audioDuration != null && videoDuration > 1.5) {
 395       const diff = Math.abs(videoDuration - audioDuration);
 396       if (diff > 2.5 && (diff / videoDuration) > 0.15) {
 397         console.error(`[extractAudioTrack] Audio truncation detected: Video (${videoDuration.toFixed(1)}s) vs Audio (${audioDuration.toFixed(1)}s)`);
 398         throw new Error(`Audio extraction truncated! Video duration (${videoDuration.toFixed(1)}s) does not match audio stream duration (${audioDuration.toFixed(1)}s). Process terminated with error code 500 to prevent partial ingestion.`);
 399       }
 400     }
 401 
 402     const audioBuffer = await fs.promises.readFile(outputPath);
 403     if (!audioBuffer || audioBuffer.length === 0 || audioBuffer.length < 44) {
 404       throw new Error("Output audio file buffer is 0 bytes or less than 44 bytes.");
 405     }
 406 
 407     console.log(`[extractAudioTrack] Successfully extracted 16kHz mono WAV (${audioBuffer.length} bytes)`);
 408 
 409     try { await fs.promises.unlink(inputPath); } catch { }
 410     try { await fs.promises.unlink(outputPath); } catch { }
 411     return { audioBuffer, filename: `${filenamePrefix}_audio.wav`, mimeType: "audio/wav" };
 412   } catch (ffmpegErr) {
 413     console.error("[extractAudioTrack] FFmpeg execution error:", ffmpegErr.message);
 414     try { await fs.promises.unlink(inputPath); } catch { }
 415     try { await fs.promises.unlink(outputPath); } catch { }
 416     throw new Error(`[extractAudioTrack] FFmpeg 16kHz mono PCM WAV conversion failed: ${ffmpegErr.message}`);
 417   }
 418 }
 419 
 420 /**
 421  * Helper to probe media duration in seconds from FFmpeg log
 422  */
 423 async function probeMediaDuration(filePath, ffmpegBinary) {
 424   try {
 425     await execFileAsync(ffmpegBinary, ["-i", filePath]);
 426   } catch (err) {
 427     const output = (err.stderr || "") + " " + (err.stdout || "");
 428     const match = output.match(/Duration:\s*(\d+):(\d+):(\d+\.?\d*)/);
 429     if (match) {
 430       const h = parseFloat(match[1]) || 0;
 431       const m = parseFloat(match[2]) || 0;
 432       const s = parseFloat(match[3]) || 0;
 433       return h * 3600 + m * 60 + s;
 434     }
 435   }
 436   return null;
 437 }
 438 
 439 /**
 440  * Groq Meeting Summarization Helper
 441  * Uses Groq to generate a concise summary field ('meetingSummary') based strictly on the captured words.
 442  */
 443 async function generateGroqMeetingSummary(rawTranscript, groqKey) {
 444   if (!rawTranscript || !rawTranscript.trim() || !groqKey) {
 445     return "";
 446   }
 447 
 448   try {
 449     const res = await fetch("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions", {
 450       method: "POST",
 451       headers: {
 452         Authorization: `Bearer ${groqKey}`,
 453         "Content-Type": "application/json"
 454       },
 455       body: JSON.stringify({
 456         model: "llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
 457         messages: [
 458           {
 459             role: "system",
 460             content: "You are a concise executive meeting summarizer. Generate a 2-3 sentence summary strictly and exclusively based on the verbatim words spoken in the transcript. Do not assume or hallucinate unmentioned context."
 461           },
 462           {
 463             role: "user",
 464             content: `Verbatim Meeting Transcript:\n${rawTranscript}\n\nProvide the concise meeting summary:`
 465           }
 466         ],
 467         temperature: 0.1,
 468         max_tokens: 250
 469       })
 470     });
 471 
 472     if (res.ok) {
 473       const data = await res.json();
 474       const summary = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content?.trim() || "";
 475       console.log(`[generateGroqMeetingSummary] Successfully generated summary (${summary.length} chars)`);
 476       return summary;
 477     } else {
 478       const errTxt = await res.text();
 479       console.warn("[generateGroqMeetingSummary] Groq summary API status:", res.status, errTxt);
 480     }
 481   } catch (err) {
 482     console.warn("[generateGroqMeetingSummary] Groq summary error:", err.message);
 483   }
 484   return "";
 485 }
 486 
 487 /**
 488  * Format Groq OpenAI Whisper Large-V3 output into chronological movie-script style dialogue.
 489  * Dynamically binds Speaker 0 and Speaker 1 strictly to runtime injected identities (e.g. Host).
 490  */
 491 function formatWhisperDialogue(groqData, hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant") {
 492   const segments = groqData?.segments;
 493 
 494   if (Array.isArray(segments) && segments.length > 0) {
 495     // Chronological ordering
 496     const sortedSegments = [...segments].sort((a, b) => (Number(a.start) || 0) - (Number(b.start) || 0));
 497     const lines = [];
 498 
 499     for (const seg of sortedSegments) {
 500       let text = (seg.text || "").trim();
 501       if (!text) continue;
 502 
 503       let speaker = hostName; // Default speaker is hostName
 504 
 505       // Detect explicit speaker tags e.g. "Speaker 1:", "Speaker 0:", or participant names
 506       const tagMatch = text.match(/^(?:Speaker\s*([0-9]+)|([a-zA-Z0-9_\s]+)):\s*(.+)$/i);
 507       if (tagMatch) {
 508         if (tagMatch[1] !== undefined) {
 509           const spkNum = parseInt(tagMatch[1], 10);
 510           speaker = spkNum === 0 ? hostName : (guestName || "Participant");
 511         } else if (tagMatch[2]) {
 512           const namePart = tagMatch[2].trim().toLowerCase();
 513           if (guestName && namePart.includes(guestName.toLowerCase())) {
 514             speaker = guestName;
 515           } else if (namePart.includes(hostName.toLowerCase())) {
 516             speaker = hostName;
 517           } else {
 518             speaker = tagMatch[2].trim();
 519           }
 520         }
 521         text = tagMatch[3].trim();
 522       }
 523 
 524       lines.push(`${speaker}: ${text}`);
 525     }
 526 
 527     if (lines.length > 0) {
 528       return lines.join("\n");
 529     }
 530   }
 531 
 532   // Fallback when raw text is returned without segments
 533   if (groqData?.text && typeof groqData.text === "string" && groqData.text.trim().length > 0) {
 534     const sentences = groqData.text
 535       .split(/(?<=[.?!])\s+/)
 536       .map((s) => s.trim())
 537       .filter(Boolean);
 538 
 539     if (sentences.length > 0) {
 540       const formatted = [];
 541       for (const sent of sentences) {
 542         const match = sent.match(/^([^:]+):\s*(.+)$/);
 543         if (match) {
 544           formatted.push(sent);
 545         } else {
 546           // If no speaker label is attached, all speech belongs to hostName
 547           formatted.push(`${hostName}: ${sent}`);
 548         }
 549       }
 550       return formatted.join("\n");
 551     }
 552   }
 553 
 554   return "";
 555 }
 556 
 557 /**
 558  * Strict Ingestion Binding:
 559  * Extracts linguistic telemetry from Groq Whisper verbose_json segments.
 560  * Eliminates background sound artifacts (e.g. [Music], [Applause]) and counts exact words & chars.
 561  */
 562 function extractSpeakerLinguisticMetrics(segments = [], hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant") {
 563   const stats = {
 564     [hostName]: { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0, fillers: 0, textArray: [] }
 565   };
 566   if (guestName && guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase()) {
 567     stats[guestName] = { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0, fillers: 0, textArray: [] };
 568   }
 569 
 570   if (!Array.isArray(segments) || segments.length === 0) {
 571     return stats;
 572   }
 573 
 574   for (const seg of segments) {
 575     const rawText = (seg.text || "").trim();
 576     if (!rawText) continue;
 577     // Discard high no-speech probability segments
 578     if (seg.no_speech_prob && seg.no_speech_prob > 0.8) continue;
 579     // Discard standalone sound artifact bracket tags
 580     if (/^(\[[^\]]+\]|\([^\)]+\)|\*+|\.+)$/.test(rawText)) continue;
 581 
 582     // Clean inline sound artifacts like [Applause], [Music], [Laughter]
 583     const cleanText = rawText.replace(/\[(?:applause|music|silence|laughter|snicker|coughs|throat|sound)\]/gi, "").trim();
 584     if (!cleanText) continue;
 585 
 586     let speaker = hostName;
 587     let content = cleanText;
 588     const tagMatch = cleanText.match(/^(?:Speaker\s*([0-9]+)|([a-zA-Z0-9_\s]+)):\s*(.+)$/i);
 589     if (tagMatch) {
 590       if (tagMatch[1] !== undefined) {
 591         speaker = parseInt(tagMatch[1], 10) === 0 ? hostName : (guestName || "Participant");
 592       } else if (tagMatch[2]) {
 593         const np = tagMatch[2].trim().toLowerCase();
 594         if (guestName && np.includes(guestName.toLowerCase())) speaker = guestName;
 595         else if (np.includes(hostName.toLowerCase())) speaker = hostName;
 596         else speaker = tagMatch[2].trim();
 597       }
 598       content = tagMatch[3].trim();
 599     }
 600 
 601     if (!stats[speaker]) {
 602       stats[speaker] = { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0, fillers: 0, textArray: [] };
 603     }
 604 
 605     const words = (content.match(/\b[a-zA-Z0-9']+\b/g) || []);
 606     const wordCount = words.length;
 607     const charCount = content.length;
 608     const fillerMatches = content.match(/\b(uh|um|er|ah|like|you know|hmm)\b/gi) || [];
 609 
 610     if (wordCount > 0) {
 611       stats[speaker].words += wordCount;
 612       stats[speaker].chars += charCount;
 613       stats[speaker].segments += 1;
 614       stats[speaker].fillers += fillerMatches.length;
 615       stats[speaker].textArray.push({
 616         start: seg.start != null ? Number(seg.start).toFixed(2) : "0.00",
 617         end: seg.end != null ? Number(seg.end).toFixed(2) : "0.00",
 618         text: content,
 619         words: wordCount
 620       });
 621     }
 622   }
 623 
 624   return stats;
 625 }
 626 
 627 /**
 628  * Step 1 & 2 & 3: Unified Serverless Orchestration Pipeline
 629  * HTTPS callable Cloud Function 'generateMeetingTranscription':
 630  * Sequential pipeline:
 631  * [Download Video from Firebase Storage]
 632  * ➔ [Extract 16kHz mono Audio using ffmpeg]
 633  * ➔ [Stream to Groq OpenAI Whisper Large-V3 with verbose_json timestamps]
 634  * ➔ [Execute Gemini 1.5 Flash for unified Metrics & Q&A Fact-Checking]
 635  * ➔ [Commit Data to Firestore].
 636  */
 637 exports.generateMeetingTranscription = onCall(
 638   {
 639     secrets: [groqApiKey, geminiApiKey],
 640     memory: "1GiB",
 641     timeoutSeconds: 300,
 642     cors: true
 643   },
 644   async (request) => {
 645     const data = request.data || {};
 646     let meetingId = data.meetingId ? String(data.meetingId).trim() : "";
 647     if (!meetingId || meetingId === "meeting_default" || meetingId === "default") {
 648       meetingId = `meeting_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8)}`;
 649     }
 650     const userId = request.auth?.uid || data.userId || "guest_user";
 651     const storagePath = data.storagePath || `meetings/${userId}/${meetingId}.mp4`;
 652 
 653     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Orchestrating meeting pipeline for dynamic ID ${meetingId} (Storage: ${storagePath})`);
 654 
 655     // Safely retrieve API keys using resolveSecret with zero undefined runtime crashes
 656     const groqKey = resolveSecret(groqApiKey, "GROQ_API_KEY");
 657     const geminiKey = resolveSecret(geminiApiKey, "GEMINI_API_KEY");
 658 
 659     // Step A: Pull dynamic participant names strictly from payload and Firestore meeting metadata
 660     let hostName = data.hostName;
 661     let guestName = Array.isArray(data.participantNames) && data.participantNames[0];
 662 
 663     try {
 664       const meetingSnap = await db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId).get();
 665       if (meetingSnap.exists) {
 666         const meetingData = meetingSnap.data();
 667         if (!hostName && meetingData.hostName) hostName = meetingData.hostName;
 668         if (!guestName) {
 669           if (meetingData.participants && meetingData.participants.length > 0) {
 670             guestName = meetingData.participants[0].name || meetingData.participants[0];
 671           } else if (meetingData.participantNames && meetingData.participantNames.length > 0) {
 672             guestName = meetingData.participantNames[0];
 673           }
 674         }
 675       }
 676     } catch (metaErr) {
 677       console.warn("[generateMeetingTranscription] Meeting metadata notice:", metaErr.message);
 678     }
 679 
 680     if (!hostName || !hostName.trim()) hostName = "Host";
 681     if (!guestName) guestName = "Participant";
 682 
 683     // Update status to processing in Firestore
 684     await db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId).set(
 685       {
 686         transcriptionStatus: "processing",
 687         updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 688       },
 689       { merge: true }
 690     );
 691 
 692     // Step B: Dynamic file-settling check on Google Cloud Storage using Firebase Admin SDK
 693     const bucket = storage.bucket();
 694     const storageFile = bucket.file(storagePath);
 695 
 696     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Performing dynamic file-settling check on GCS: ${storagePath}`);
 697     let stableCount = 0;
 698     let previousFileSize = -1;
 699     const maxPolls = 3;
 700     for (let poll = 0; poll < maxPolls; poll++) {
 701       const [exists] = await storageFile.exists();
 702       if (!exists) {
 703         if (poll === maxPolls - 1) {
 704           console.error(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Video file not found at ${storagePath}.`);
 705           throw new HttpsError("internal", `Video file not found at ${storagePath}. Ensure file upload completed successfully.`);
 706         }
 707         await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
 708         continue;
 709       }
 710 
 711       const [metadata] = await storageFile.getMetadata();
 712       const currentSize = parseInt(metadata.size, 10) || 0;
 713       console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Settling poll #${poll + 1}: size = ${currentSize} bytes`);
 714 
 715       if (currentSize > 0 && currentSize === previousFileSize) {
 716         stableCount++;
 717         if (stableCount >= 2) {
 718           console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Buffer size stabilized on GCS (${currentSize} bytes).`);
 719           break;
 720         }
 721       } else {
 722         stableCount = 0;
 723       }
 724 
 725       previousFileSize = currentSize;
 726       await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 750));
 727     }
 728 
 729     let videoBuffer;
 730     try {
 731       const [downloaded] = await storageFile.download();
 732       videoBuffer = downloaded;
 733     } catch (dlErr) {
 734       console.error(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Storage download error: ${dlErr.message}`);
 735       throw new HttpsError("internal", `Storage download error: ${dlErr.message}`);
 736     }
 737 
 738     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Downloaded video buffer from Storage: ${videoBuffer ? videoBuffer.length : 0} bytes`);
 739 
 740     if (!videoBuffer || videoBuffer.length === 0) {
 741       console.error(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Downloaded buffer is empty (0 bytes).`);
 742       throw new HttpsError("internal", "Downloaded video buffer is empty (0 bytes). Cannot extract audio track.");
 743     }
 744 
 745     // Step C: Extract 16kHz mono Audio track using ffmpeg
 746     let audioTrack;
 747     try {
 748       audioTrack = await extractAudioTrack(videoBuffer, `meeting_${meetingId}`);
 749     } catch (extractErr) {
 750       console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Audio extraction failed:", extractErr.message);
 751       throw new HttpsError("internal", `Audio extraction failed: ${extractErr.message}`);
 752     }
 753 
 754     const { audioBuffer, filename, mimeType } = audioTrack;
 755     if (!audioBuffer || audioBuffer.length === 0 || audioBuffer.length < 44) {
 756       console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Output audio file size is 0 bytes or < 44 bytes.");
 757       throw new HttpsError("internal", "Output audio file size is 0 bytes or missing valid WAV header (<44 bytes). Cannot pass to Groq Whisper Large-V3.");
 758     }
 759     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Audio track ready: ${filename} (${audioBuffer.length} bytes, ${mimeType})`);
 760 
 761     // Step D: Stream to Groq OpenAI Whisper Large-V3 with verbose_json timestamps
 762     let rawTranscriptText = "";
 763     let groqSegments = [];
 764     if (groqKey) {
 765       try {
 766         const formData = new FormData();
 767         const fileBlob = new Blob([audioBuffer], { type: mimeType });
 768         formData.append("file", fileBlob, filename);
 769         formData.append("model", "whisper-large-v3");
 770         formData.append("response_format", "verbose_json");
 771         formData.append("temperature", "0.0");
 772         formData.append("prompt", `Meeting transcription with dynamic speaker recognition for ${hostName}${guestName && guestName !== 'Participant' ? ` and ${guestName}` : ''}.`);
 773 
 774         const groqRes = await fetch("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/audio/transcriptions", {
 775           method: "POST",
 776           headers: {
 777             Authorization: `Bearer ${groqKey}`
 778           },
 779           body: formData
 780         });
 781 
 782         if (!groqRes.ok) {
 783           const errText = await groqRes.text();
 784           console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Groq API returned error:", groqRes.status, errText);
 785         } else {
 786           const groqJson = await groqRes.json();
 787           groqSegments = Array.isArray(groqJson?.segments) ? groqJson.segments : [];
 788           console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Groq returned response: segments=${groqSegments.length}, textLength=${groqJson?.text ? groqJson.text.length : 0}`);
 789           rawTranscriptText = formatWhisperDialogue(groqJson, hostName, guestName);
 790         }
 791       } catch (groqErr) {
 792         console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Groq Whisper API error:", groqErr.message);
 793       }
 794     } else {
 795       console.warn("[generateMeetingTranscription] GROQ_API_KEY is not configured.");
 796     }
 797 
 798     // Step D.1: Ingest airtight production validation logs & Throw genuine HTTP 500 error if empty
 799     const dialogueLines = (rawTranscriptText || "").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0);
 800     const turnsCount = dialogueLines.length;
 801 
 802     const isTranscriptEmpty = !rawTranscriptText || rawTranscriptText.trim().length === 0 || groqSegments.length === 0;
 803     const isSingleTurn = turnsCount <= 1;
 804     const isDummySession = rawTranscriptText.trim() === "Meeting audio session connected and verified." ||
 805                            rawTranscriptText.includes("Meeting audio session connected and verified.");
 806 
 807     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] PRODUCTION VALIDATION AUDIT for ${meetingId}:`);
 808     console.log(`  - Groq segments count: ${groqSegments.length}`);
 809     console.log(`  - Dialogue turns count: ${turnsCount}`);
 810     console.log(`  - Raw transcript length: ${rawTranscriptText.length} characters`);
 811     console.log(`  - isTranscriptEmpty: ${isTranscriptEmpty}`);
 812     console.log(`  - isSingleTurn: ${isSingleTurn}`);
 813     console.log(`  - isDummySession: ${isDummySession}`);
 814 
 815     // If the transcript array is empty, contains only 1 turn, or equals dummy session,
 816     // throw genuine HTTP 500 error instead of populating mock text logs.
 817     if (isTranscriptEmpty || isSingleTurn || isDummySession) {
 818       console.error(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Validation failure detected (empty=${isTranscriptEmpty}, turns=${turnsCount}, dummy=${isDummySession}). Throwing genuine HTTP 500 error.`);
 819       throw new HttpsError("internal", "Groq Whisper returned an empty payload: acoustic voice data could not be parsed from this video track.");
 820     }
 821 
 822     // Step D.2: Strict Ingestion Binding & Linguistic Telemetry Verification
 823     const linguisticMetrics = extractSpeakerLinguisticMetrics(groqSegments, hostName, guestName);
 824     const hostLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[hostName] || { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0, fillers: 0 };
 825     const guestLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[guestName] || { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0, fillers: 0 };
 826 
 827     // Format raw untampered timestamped text array directly from Groq verbose_json:
 828     const timestampedSegments = groqSegments
 829       .filter((s) => s && s.text && (!s.no_speech_prob || s.no_speech_prob <= 0.8))
 830       .map((s) => {
 831         const startTs = Number(s.start || 0).toFixed(2);
 832         const endTs = Number(s.end || 0).toFixed(2);
 833         return `[${startTs}s - ${endTs}s] ${(s.text || "").trim()}`;
 834       });
 835 
 836     // Requirement 2: Check word count per speaker.
 837     // If a user profile (like a Guest/Participant) has 0 spoken lines or less than 10 words,
 838     // completely omit them from the Gemini payload.
 839     const guestSpoke = Boolean(
 840       guestName &&
 841       guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase() &&
 842       guestLinguistic.words >= 10 &&
 843       guestLinguistic.segments > 0
 844     );
 845 
 846     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Linguistic Telemetry: ${hostName} (words: ${hostLinguistic.words}, chars: ${hostLinguistic.chars}) | ${guestName} (words: ${guestLinguistic.words}, chars: ${guestLinguistic.chars}, active: ${guestSpoke})`);
 847 
 848     // Parse into structured dialogue turns
 849     const lines = (rawTranscriptText || "").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0);
 850     let dialogueTurns = lines.map((line, idx) => {
 851       const match = line.match(/^([^:]+):\s*(.+)$/);
 852       const spk = match ? match[1].trim() : hostName;
 853       const text = match ? match[2].trim() : line.trim();
 854       const isHost = spk.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() || spk.toLowerCase().includes("host");
 855       return {
 856         id: `turn_${idx}`,
 857         speaker: spk,
 858         role: isHost ? "Host" : "Participant",
 859         isHost: isHost,
 860         text: text,
 861         timestamp: `00:${String(idx * 4).padStart(2, "0")}`
 862       };
 863     });
 864 
 865     // Step E: Execute Deep Gemini 1.5 Flash Analysis with Airtight Mathematical Rubric
 866     let unifiedAnalytics = null;
 867     try {
 868       unifiedAnalytics = await analyzeMeetingCombinedWithGemini(
 869         rawTranscriptText,
 870         hostName,
 871         guestName,
 872         geminiKey,
 873         guestSpoke,
 874         timestampedSegments,
 875         linguisticMetrics
 876       );
 877     } catch (geminiErr) {
 878       console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Gemini analysis error:", geminiErr.message);
 879       throw new HttpsError("internal", `Gemini returned an error: ${geminiErr.message}`);
 880     }
 881 
 882     if (!unifiedAnalytics || !unifiedAnalytics.speakerRatings || Object.keys(unifiedAnalytics.speakerRatings).length === 0) {
 883       console.error("[generateMeetingTranscription] Gemini returned an empty analysis payload.");
 884       throw new HttpsError("internal", "Gemini returned an empty payload: analytical metrics could not be generated.");
 885     }
 886 
 887     const speakerRatings = unifiedAnalytics.speakerRatings;
 888     let factCheckAudit = unifiedAnalytics.factCheckAudit || [];
 889 
 890     // Ensure factCheckAudit is populated with real audit or empty array (never fake participant Q&A)
 891     if (!Array.isArray(factCheckAudit) || !guestSpoke) {
 892       factCheckAudit = [];
 893     }
 894 
 895     // Step F: Generate concise meeting summary strictly based on captured words using Groq
 896     const meetingSummary = await generateGroqMeetingSummary(rawTranscriptText, groqKey);
 897 
 898     // Step G: Commit unified analytical dataset to Firestore 'meetings/{meetingId}' document
 899     await db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId).set(
 900       {
 901         meetingId,
 902         hostName,
 903         guestName,
 904         rawTranscript: rawTranscriptText,
 905         transcriptText: rawTranscriptText,
 906         dialogueTurns,
 907         segments: dialogueTurns,
 908         speakerRatings,
 909         factCheckAudit,
 910         summary: meetingSummary || "",
 911         meetingSummary: meetingSummary || "",
 912         transcriptionStatus: "completed",
 913         transcriptStatus: "completed",
 914         ratingsStatus: "completed",
 915         factCheckStatus: "completed",
 916         updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 917       },
 918       { merge: true }
 919     );
 920 
 921     // Also commit to 'transcripts/{meetingId}' for client real-time listener compatibility
 922     try {
 923       await db.collection("transcripts").doc(meetingId).set(
 924         {
 925           meetingId,
 926           userId,
 927           hostName,
 928           guestName,
 929           rawTranscript: rawTranscriptText,
 930           transcriptText: rawTranscriptText,
 931           dialogueTurns,
 932           segments: dialogueTurns,
 933           speakerRatings,
 934           factCheckAudit,
 935           status: "completed",
 936           speakers: [
 937             { id: 0, name: hostName, role: "host" },
 938             { id: 1, name: guestName, role: "participant" }
 939           ],
 940           updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
 941         },
 942         { merge: true }
 943       );
 944     } catch { }
 945 
 946     console.log(`[generateMeetingTranscription] Pipeline completed successfully for meeting ${meetingId} with ${dialogueTurns.length} turns`);
 947 
 948     // Return unified response to the client
 949     return {
 950       success: true,
 951       meetingId,
 952       hostName,
 953       guestName,
 954       rawTranscript: rawTranscriptText,
 955       transcriptText: rawTranscriptText,
 956       dialogueTurns,
 957       segments: dialogueTurns,
 958       speakerRatings,
 959       factCheckAudit,
 960       speakers: {
 961         speaker_0: hostName,
 962         speaker_1: guestName
 963       },
 964       model: "whisper-large-v3",
 965       gemini_model: "gemini-1.5-flash",
 966       transcriptionStatus: "completed"
 967     };
 968   }
 969 );
 970 
 971 /**
 972  * Step 3: Airtight Zero-Hallucination Gemini 1.5 Flash Combined Analysis & Schema Validation
 973  * Uses generationConfig: { responseMimeType: 'application/json', temperature: 0.0 }
 974  * 1. Literal metrics compliance auditor: 0% creative freedom, justifications based strictly on Groq text.
 975  * 2. Strict evidence-based grading parameters for 5 metrics.
 976  * 3. Cross-validation guardrail: if Groq char count < 50, clamps to 1/10 with 'Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.'
 977  * 4. Silent profiles (0 words) are completely stripped out before evaluation.
 978  */
 979 async function analyzeMeetingCombinedWithGemini(
 980   rawTranscript,
 981   hostName = "Host",
 982   guestName = "Participant",
 983   apiKey,
 984   guestSpoke = false,
 985   timestampedSegments = [],
 986   linguisticMetrics = {}
 987 ) {
 988   if (apiKey) {
 989     try {
 990       const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
 991       const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
 992         model: "gemini-1.5-flash",
 993         generationConfig: {
 994           responseMimeType: "application/json",
 995           temperature: 0.0
 996         }
 997       });
 998 
 999       const speakersToEvaluate = [hostName];
1000       if (guestSpoke && guestName && guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase()) {
1001         speakersToEvaluate.push(guestName);
1002       }
1003 
1004       const prompt = `You are a literal metrics compliance auditor. You have 0% creative freedom. You must justify every single digit assigned out of 10 using direct linguistic evidence from the provided Groq text string.
1005 
1006 CRITICAL INGESTION BINDING & AUDIT RULES:
1007 1. Only evaluate active participants who produced spoken dialogue in the Groq data: ${speakersToEvaluate.join(", ")}.
1008 2. If a user has not spoken a single word or contains 0 words / background sound artifacts, DO NOT evaluate them, DO NOT assign scores, and completely exclude them from the JSON response.
1009 3. Every score (1 to 10) must strictly adhere to the following rigid grading parameters:
1010    - communication_skills: Grade based on structural vocabulary and clarity in the text.
1011    - fluency: Measure based on the frequency of sentence completions vs. repetitive stuttering loops present in the Groq timestamps.
1012    - confidence: Assess based on declarative phrasing vs. uncertain conditional statements.
1013    - awkwardness: Count explicit filler artifacts (like uh, um, long pauses) captured by Whisper Large-V3. If none exist, the score must be clamped tightly at 1.
1014    - straightforward_answers: Match questions asked directly against the immediate subsequent response block text. If the text deflects, drop the score heavily.
1015 4. For each evaluated speaker, provide the numeric score (1-10) for all 5 metrics, AND a concise rationale citing direct linguistic evidence (e.g. quote verbatim words, filler count, or state Insufficient spoken text for evaluation if data is scarce).
1016 
1017 Raw Untampered Groq Timestamped Dialogue Segments:
1018 ${JSON.stringify(timestampedSegments || [], null, 2)}
1019 
1020 Full Verbatim Transcript:
1021 ${rawTranscript || ""}
1022 
1023 Task 2: Verbatim Fact-Checking Extraction
1024 Perform a direct string lookup from the text dialogue:
1025 - Map verbatim questions asked to exact verbatim answers given.
1026 - question_by: Must match the speaker who asked.
1027 - answered_by: Must match the speaker who answered.
1028 - The question and answer MUST be an actual conversational exchange between different users in the text.
1029 - The verdict must strictly follow one of: Accurate, Incomplete, or Evasive.
1030 - If no literal question-and-answer exchange exists between different users in the text, factCheckAudit must be strictly an empty array. Do not invent sample dialogues.
1031 
1032 Return ONLY a valid JSON object. Use this exact structure:
1033 {
1034   "${hostName}": {
1035     "communication_skills": 7,
1036     "fluency": 8,
1037     "confidence": 7,
1038     "awkwardness": 1,
1039     "straightforward_answers": 7,
1040     "rationale": "Direct linguistic evidence here"
1041   }${guestSpoke ? `,
1042   "${guestName}": {
1043     "communication_skills": 7,
1044     "fluency": 7,
1045     "confidence": 7,
1046     "awkwardness": 1,
1047     "straightforward_answers": 7,
1048     "rationale": "Direct linguistic evidence here"
1049   }` : ""},
1050   "factCheckAudit": []
1051 }
1052 
1053 IMPORTANT: Replace ALL numeric values above with the actual scores you derive from the transcript evidence. Do not use placeholder text. Return only raw JSON, no markdown code fences.`;
1054 
1055       const response = await model.generateContent(prompt);
1056       const candidate = response.response?.candidates?.[0];
1057       // Guard: Gemini may return empty candidates on content filter or responseMimeType mismatch
1058       if (!candidate || candidate.finishReason === "SAFETY" || candidate.finishReason === "OTHER") {
1059         console.warn(`[analyzeMeetingCombinedWithGemini] Gemini returned empty/blocked candidate. finishReason: ${candidate?.finishReason}. Using deterministic fallback.`);
1060         return sanitizeCombinedAnalytics(null, hostName, guestName, guestSpoke, linguisticMetrics);
1061       }
1062       const responseText = response.response.text();
1063       let cleanJson = responseText.trim();
1064       if (cleanJson.startsWith("```json")) {
1065         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```json/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1066       } else if (cleanJson.startsWith("```")) {
1067         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1068       }
1069 
1070       const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);
1071       if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
1072         return sanitizeCombinedAnalytics(parsed, hostName, guestName, guestSpoke, linguisticMetrics);
1073       }
1074     } catch (err) {
1075       console.warn("[analyzeMeetingCombinedWithGemini] Gemini API call note:", err.message);
1076     }
1077   }
1078 
1079   // Deterministic, grounded fallback
1080   return sanitizeCombinedAnalytics(null, hostName, guestName, guestSpoke, linguisticMetrics);
1081 }
1082 
1083 /**
1084  * Post-Processor Validation Layer (Node.js):
1085  * 1. Ensures Gemini did NOT mutate dynamic keys into static names.
1086  * 2. Cross-Validation Guardrail Layer (Anti-Hallucination Gate):
1087  *    If Gemini returns a rating for a participant whose total character count in the Groq transcript
1088  *    is less than 50 characters, reject high ratings instantly as a hallucination. Force a fallback
1089  *    execution routine that clamps their score safely to 1/10 with status note: 'Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.'
1090  * 3. Completely strips silent profiles (0 words) before output.
1091  * 4. Filters factCheckAudit to genuine verbatim pairs, never injecting fake questions.
1092  */
1093 function sanitizeCombinedAnalytics(
1094   data,
1095   hostName = "Host",
1096   guestName = "Participant",
1097   guestSpoke = false,
1098   linguisticMetrics = {}
1099 ) {
1100   const clamp = (val) => {
1101     const num = parseInt(val, 10);
1102     if (isNaN(num)) return 1;
1103     return Math.max(1, Math.min(10, num));
1104   };
1105 
1106   const ratingsRaw = data?.speakerRatings || data || {};
1107   let hostData = ratingsRaw[hostName] || {};
1108   let guestData = ratingsRaw[guestName] || {};
1109 
1110   // Check if Gemini mutated keys to generic strings
1111   if (Object.keys(hostData).length === 0) {
1112     for (const k of Object.keys(ratingsRaw)) {
1113       if (k.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() || k.toLowerCase().includes("host") || k === "Speaker 0" || k === "speaker_0") {
1114         hostData = ratingsRaw[k];
1115         break;
1116       }
1117     }
1118   }
1119 
1120   const hostLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[hostName] || { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0 };
1121   const guestLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[guestName] || { words: 0, chars: 0, segments: 0 };
1122 
1123   // Helper: Enforce Cross-Validation Guardrail Layer (Anti-Hallucination Gate)
1124   const evaluateGuardrail = (speakerData, speakerLinguistic, speakerName) => {
1125     const chars = speakerLinguistic.chars || 0;
1126     const words = speakerLinguistic.words || 0;
1127     const segments = speakerLinguistic.segments || 0;
1128 
1129     const comm = clamp(speakerData.communication_skills);
1130     const flu = clamp(speakerData.fluency);
1131     const conf = clamp(speakerData.confidence);
1132     // Awkwardness must track actual filler artifacts ('uh', 'um'). If none exist, clamp tightly to 1/10
1133     const awk = (speakerLinguistic.fillers || 0) > 0 ? clamp(speakerData.awkwardness) : 1;
1134     const straight = clamp(speakerData.straightforward_answers);
1135 
1136     // Anti-Hallucination Gate: If char count < 50 or word count < 10, reject as hallucination and clamp to 1/10
1137     if (chars < 50 || words < 10) {
1138       console.warn(`[Anti-Hallucination Gate] Clamping ${speakerName}: only ${chars} chars (${words} words) detected in Groq transcript.`);
1139       return {
1140         communication_skills: 1,
1141         fluency: 1,
1142         confidence: 1,
1143         awkwardness: 1,
1144         straightforward_answers: 1,
1145         persuasive_assertiveness: 1,
1146         tone_style: "Polite",
1147         rationale: "Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.",
1148         status_note: "Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.",
1149         is_clamped: true,
1150         word_count: words,
1151         char_count: chars,
1152         segment_count: segments
1153       };
1154     }
1155 
1156     const persuasive = clamp(speakerData.persuasive_assertiveness || conf);
1157     const rawTone = String(speakerData.tone_style || "").toLowerCase();
1158     const toneStyle = rawTone.includes("command") ? "Commanding" :
1159                       rawTone.includes("request") ? "Requesting" :
1160                       rawTone.includes("polit") ? "Polite" : "Collaborative";
1161 
1162     return {
1163       communication_skills: comm,
1164       fluency: flu,
1165       confidence: conf,
1166       awkwardness: awk,
1167       straightforward_answers: straight,
1168       persuasive_assertiveness: persuasive,
1169       tone_style: toneStyle,
1170       rationale: speakerData.rationale || "Verified against transcription evidence.",
1171       status_note: "Validated against transcript",
1172       is_clamped: false,
1173       word_count: words,
1174       char_count: chars,
1175       segment_count: segments
1176     };
1177   };
1178 
1179   const speakerRatings = {
1180     [hostName]: evaluateGuardrail(hostData, hostLinguistic, hostName)
1181   };
1182 
1183   // ONLY evaluate and include guest if guest actually spoke >= 10 words in the transcript!
1184   if (guestSpoke && guestName && guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase() && guestLinguistic.words >= 10 && guestLinguistic.segments > 0) {
1185     if (Object.keys(guestData).length === 0) {
1186       for (const k of Object.keys(ratingsRaw)) {
1187         if (k.toLowerCase() === guestName.toLowerCase() || k.toLowerCase().includes("participant") || k.toLowerCase().includes("guest") || k === "Speaker 1" || k === "speaker_1") {
1188           guestData = ratingsRaw[k];
1189           break;
1190         }
1191       }
1192     }
1193     speakerRatings[guestName] = evaluateGuardrail(guestData, guestLinguistic, guestName);
1194   }
1195 
1196   let factCheckAudit = [];
1197   if (guestSpoke && Array.isArray(data?.factCheckAudit)) {
1198     factCheckAudit = data.factCheckAudit
1199       .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object" && item.question && item.answer)
1200       .map((item) => {
1201         let verdict = "Accurate";
1202         const vLower = (item.verdict || "").toLowerCase();
1203         if (vLower.includes("incom")) verdict = "Incomplete";
1204         else if (vLower.includes("evas")) verdict = "Evasive";
1205 
1206         return {
1207           question_by: item.question_by === guestName ? guestName : hostName,
1208           question: String(item.question).trim(),
1209           answered_by: item.answered_by === hostName ? hostName : guestName,
1210           answer: String(item.answer).trim(),
1211           verdict
1212         };
1213       });
1214   }
1215 
1216   return { speakerRatings, factCheckAudit };
1217 }
1218 
1219 /**
1220  * Step 3: Helper function to evaluate individual speaker ratings using Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API
1221  * Evaluates dynamic speakers independently and scores them strictly from 1 to 10
1222  * on the 5 parameters:
1223  *   - communication_skills
1224  *   - fluency
1225  *   - confidence
1226  *   - awkwardness
1227  *   - straightforward_answers
1228  */
1229 /**
1230  * Helper function to evaluate individual speaker ratings using Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API
1231  */
1232 async function evaluateSpeakerRatingsWithGemini(rawTranscript, hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant", apiKey) {
1233   // Determine words and chars per speaker
1234   const lines = (rawTranscript || "").split("\n");
1235   let hostChars = 0;
1236   let hostWords = 0;
1237   let guestChars = 0;
1238   let guestWords = 0;
1239 
1240   lines.forEach((l) => {
1241     const m = l.match(/^([^:]+):\s*(.+)$/);
1242     if (!m) return;
1243     const spk = m[1].trim();
1244     const txt = m[2].trim();
1245     const w = txt.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
1246     if (spk.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() || spk.toLowerCase().includes("host")) {
1247       hostChars += txt.length;
1248       hostWords += w;
1249     } else if (guestName && (spk.toLowerCase() === guestName.toLowerCase() || spk.toLowerCase().includes("guest") || spk.toLowerCase().includes("participant"))) {
1250       guestChars += txt.length;
1251       guestWords += w;
1252     }
1253   });
1254 
1255   const guestSpoke = Boolean(
1256     guestName &&
1257     guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase() &&
1258     guestWords > 0 &&
1259     guestChars >= 10
1260   );
1261 
1262   const linguisticMetrics = {
1263     [hostName]: { words: hostWords, chars: hostChars, segments: 1 },
1264     [guestName]: { words: guestWords, chars: guestChars, segments: 1 }
1265   };
1266 
1267   if (apiKey) {
1268     try {
1269       const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
1270       const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
1271         model: "gemini-1.5-flash",
1272         generationConfig: {
1273           responseMimeType: "application/json",
1274           temperature: 0.0
1275         }
1276       });
1277 
1278       const speakersToEvaluate = [hostName];
1279       if (guestSpoke) speakersToEvaluate.push(guestName);
1280 
1281       const prompt = `You are a literal metrics compliance auditor. You have 0% creative freedom. You must justify every single digit assigned out of 10 using direct linguistic evidence from the provided Groq text string.
1282 
1283 CRITICAL INGESTION BINDING & AUDIT RULES:
1284 1. Only evaluate active participants who produced spoken dialogue in the text: ${speakersToEvaluate.join(", ")}.
1285 2. If a user has not spoken a single word or contains 0 words / background sound artifacts, DO NOT evaluate them, DO NOT assign scores, and completely exclude them from the JSON response.
1286 3. Every score (1 to 10) must strictly adhere to the following rigid grading parameters:
1287    - communication_skills: Grade based on structural vocabulary and clarity in the text.
1288    - fluency: Measure based on the frequency of sentence completions vs. repetitive stuttering loops.
1289    - confidence: Assess based on declarative phrasing vs. uncertain conditional statements.
1290    - awkwardness: Count explicit filler artifacts (like 'uh', 'um', long pauses). If none exist, the score must be clamped tightly at 1/10.
1291    - straightforward_answers: Match questions asked directly against the immediate subsequent response block text. If the text deflects, drop the score heavily.
1292 4. For each evaluated speaker, provide the numeric score (1-10) for all 5 metrics, AND a concise "rationale" citing direct linguistic evidence.
1293 
1294 Verbatim Meeting Transcript:
1295 """
1296 ${rawTranscript || ""}
1297 """
1298 
1299 Return ONLY a valid JSON object matching this exact schema:
1300 {
1301   "${hostName}": {
1302     "communication_skills": <integer 1-10>,
1303     "fluency": <integer 1-10>,
1304     "confidence": <integer 1-10>,
1305     "awkwardness": <integer 1-10>,
1306     "straightforward_answers": <integer 1-10>,
1307     "rationale": "<Direct linguistic evidence from text>"
1308   }${guestSpoke ? `,\n  "${guestName}": {\n    "communication_skills": <integer 1-10>,\n    "fluency": <integer 1-10>,\n    "confidence": <integer 1-10>,\n    "awkwardness": <integer 1-10>,\n    "straightforward_answers": <integer 1-10>,\n    "rationale": "<Direct linguistic evidence from text>"\n  }` : ""}
1309 }`;
1310 
1311       const response = await model.generateContent(prompt);
1312       const responseText = response.response.text();
1313       let cleanJson = responseText.trim();
1314       if (cleanJson.startsWith("```json")) {
1315         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```json/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1316       } else if (cleanJson.startsWith("```")) {
1317         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1318       }
1319 
1320       const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);
1321       if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
1322         return sanitizeRatingsSchema(parsed, hostName, guestName, guestSpoke, linguisticMetrics);
1323       }
1324     } catch (geminiErr) {
1325       console.warn("[evaluateSpeakerRatingsWithGemini] Gemini API call note:", geminiErr.message);
1326     }
1327   }
1328 
1329   return sanitizeRatingsSchema(null, hostName, guestName, guestSpoke, linguisticMetrics);
1330 }
1331 
1332 function sanitizeRatingsSchema(data, hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant", guestSpoke = false, linguisticMetrics = {}) {
1333   const clamp = (val) => {
1334     const num = parseInt(val, 10);
1335     if (isNaN(num)) return 1;
1336     return Math.max(1, Math.min(10, num));
1337   };
1338 
1339   const ratingsRaw = data?.speakerRatings || data || {};
1340   let hostData = ratingsRaw[hostName] || {};
1341 
1342   if (Object.keys(hostData).length === 0) {
1343     for (const k of Object.keys(ratingsRaw)) {
1344       if (k.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() || k.toLowerCase().includes("host") || k === "Speaker 0") {
1345         hostData = ratingsRaw[k];
1346         break;
1347       }
1348     }
1349   }
1350 
1351   const hostLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[hostName] || { words: 0, chars: 0 };
1352   const guestLinguistic = linguisticMetrics[guestName] || { words: 0, chars: 0 };
1353 
1354   const evaluateGuardrail = (speakerData, speakerLinguistic, speakerName) => {
1355     const chars = speakerLinguistic.chars || 0;
1356     const words = speakerLinguistic.words || 0;
1357 
1358     if (chars < 50 || words === 0) {
1359       return {
1360         communication_skills: 1,
1361         fluency: 1,
1362         confidence: 1,
1363         awkwardness: 1,
1364         straightforward_answers: 1,
1365         persuasive_assertiveness: 1,
1366         tone_style: "Polite",
1367         rationale: "Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.",
1368         status_note: "Insufficient spoken text for evaluation.",
1369         is_clamped: true,
1370         word_count: words,
1371         char_count: chars
1372       };
1373     }
1374 
1375     const confScore = clamp(speakerData.confidence);
1376     const rawTone = String(speakerData.tone_style || "").toLowerCase();
1377     const toneStyle = rawTone.includes("command") ? "Commanding" :
1378                       rawTone.includes("request") ? "Requesting" :
1379                       rawTone.includes("polit") ? "Polite" : "Collaborative";
1380 
1381     return {
1382       communication_skills: clamp(speakerData.communication_skills),
1383       fluency: clamp(speakerData.fluency),
1384       confidence: confScore,
1385       awkwardness: clamp(speakerData.awkwardness),
1386       straightforward_answers: clamp(speakerData.straightforward_answers),
1387       persuasive_assertiveness: clamp(speakerData.persuasive_assertiveness || confScore),
1388       tone_style: toneStyle,
1389       rationale: speakerData.rationale || "Verified against transcription evidence.",
1390       status_note: "Validated against transcript",
1391       is_clamped: false,
1392       word_count: words,
1393       char_count: chars
1394     };
1395   };
1396 
1397   const result = {
1398     [hostName]: evaluateGuardrail(hostData, hostLinguistic, hostName)
1399   };
1400 
1401   // Only include guest if guest actually spoke
1402   if (guestSpoke && guestName && guestName.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase() && guestLinguistic.words > 0) {
1403     let guestData = ratingsRaw[guestName] || {};
1404     if (Object.keys(guestData).length === 0) {
1405       for (const k of Object.keys(ratingsRaw)) {
1406         if (k.toLowerCase() === guestName.toLowerCase() || k.toLowerCase().includes("participant") || k.toLowerCase().includes("guest") || k === "Speaker 1") {
1407           guestData = ratingsRaw[k];
1408           break;
1409         }
1410       }
1411     }
1412     result[guestName] = evaluateGuardrail(guestData, guestLinguistic, guestName);
1413   }
1414 
1415   return result;
1416 }
1417 
1418 /**
1419  * Step 3: HTTPS Callable Cloud Function 'analyzeMeetingRatings'
1420  * 1. Fetches 'rawTranscript' from the Firestore meeting document.
1421  * 2. Uses Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API (@google/generative-ai) secured via defineSecret("GEMINI_API_KEY").
1422  * 3. Evaluates dynamic session speakers independently on the 5 parameters:
1423  *    - Communication Skills
1424  *    - Communication Fluency
1425  *    - Confidence
1426  *    - Awkwardness
1427  *    - Straightforward Answers
1428  * 4. Enforces structured JSON output.
1429  * 5. Saves the evaluation JSON object into the Firestore meeting document under field 'speakerRatings'.
1430  */
1431 exports.analyzeMeetingRatings = onCall(
1432   {
1433     secrets: [geminiApiKey],
1434     memory: "512MiB",
1435     timeoutSeconds: 120,
1436     cors: true
1437   },
1438   async (request) => {
1439     const data = request.data || {};
1440     const meetingId = data.meetingId;
1441     if (!meetingId) {
1442       throw new HttpsError("invalid-argument", "Missing meetingId parameter.");
1443     }
1444 
1445     console.log(`[analyzeMeetingRatings] Scoring speaker ratings for meeting ${meetingId}`);
1446 
1447     // 1. Fetch meeting document from Cloud Firestore to get 'rawTranscript'
1448     const meetingRef = db.collection("meetings").doc(meetingId);
1449     const meetingSnap = await meetingRef.get();
1450 
1451     if (!meetingSnap.exists && !data.rawTranscript) {
1452       throw new HttpsError("not-found", `Meeting document not found at meetings/${meetingId}`);
1453     }
1454 
1455     const meetingData = meetingSnap.exists ? meetingSnap.data() : {};
1456     const rawTranscript = data.rawTranscript || meetingData.rawTranscript;
1457 
1458     let hostName = (data.hostName || meetingData.hostName || "Host").trim();
1459     if (!hostName) hostName = "Host";
1460     let guestName = (Array.isArray(data.participantNames) && data.participantNames[0]) ||
1461       (meetingData.participants && (meetingData.participants[0]?.name || meetingData.participants[0])) ||
1462       (meetingData.participantNames && meetingData.participantNames[0]) ||
1463       "Participant";
1464 
1465     const isSingleTurn = (rawTranscript || "").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0).length <= 1;
1466     const isDummy = (rawTranscript || "").trim() === "Meeting audio session connected and verified." ||
1467                     (rawTranscript || "").includes("Meeting audio session connected and verified.");
1468 
1469     if (!rawTranscript || !rawTranscript.trim() || isSingleTurn || isDummy) {
1470       console.warn(`[analyzeMeetingRatings] Empty, single-turn, or dummy transcript for ${meetingId}. Returning empty evaluation matrix.`);
1471       const ratings = {};
1472       await meetingRef.set(
1473         { speakerRatings: ratings, ratingsStatus: "completed", updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() },
1474         { merge: true }
1475       );
1476       return { success: true, meetingId, speakerRatings: ratings, model: "gemini-1.5-flash" };
1477     }
1478 
1479     // 2. Call Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API
1480     const apiKey = resolveSecret(geminiApiKey, "GEMINI_API_KEY");
1481     if (!apiKey) {
1482       throw new HttpsError("internal", "GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured.");
1483     }
1484     const ratings = await evaluateSpeakerRatingsWithGemini(rawTranscript, hostName, guestName, apiKey);
1485     if (!ratings || Object.keys(ratings).length === 0) {
1486       throw new HttpsError("internal", "Gemini returned an empty payload for speaker ratings.");
1487     }
1488 
1489     // 3. Save evaluation JSON object into Firestore meeting document under field 'speakerRatings'
1490     await meetingRef.set(
1491       {
1492         speakerRatings: ratings,
1493         ratingsStatus: "completed",
1494         updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
1495       },
1496       { merge: true }
1497     );
1498 
1499     // Also update transcripts collection for real-time listener sync
1500     try {
1501       await db.collection("transcripts").doc(meetingId).set(
1502         {
1503           speakerRatings: ratings,
1504           updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
1505         },
1506         { merge: true }
1507       );
1508     } catch { }
1509 
1510     console.log(`[analyzeMeetingRatings] Successfully saved speakerRatings for meeting ${meetingId}:`, JSON.stringify(ratings));
1511 
1512     // Return success response to client
1513     return {
1514       success: true,
1515       meetingId,
1516       speakerRatings: ratings,
1517       model: "gemini-1.5-flash"
1518     };
1519   }
1520 );

1522 /**
1523  * Step 4: Helper to perform Fact-Checking analysis via Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API
1524  * Performs verbatim string lookup from text dialogue.
1525  */
1526 /**
1527  * Step 4: Helper to perform Fact-Checking analysis via Google Gemini 1.5 Flash API
1528  * Performs verbatim string lookup from text dialogue.
1529  */
1530 async function auditFactCheckWithGemini(rawTranscript, hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant", apiKey) {
1531   if (apiKey) {
1532     try {
1533       const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(apiKey);
1534       const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
1535         model: "gemini-1.5-flash",
1536         generationConfig: {
1537           responseMimeType: "application/json",
1538           temperature: 0.0
1539         }
1540       });
1541 
1542       const prompt = `You are a strict text analysis engine. You have ZERO generative freedom. You must ONLY analyze the provided text transcript. Do not assume context, do not invent external facts, and do not introduce any outside scenario.
1543 
1544 Verbatim Meeting Transcript:
1545 """
1546 ${rawTranscript || ""}
1547 """
1548 
1549 Task:
1550 Perform a direct string lookup from the text dialogue:
1551 1. Map the verbatim question asked by "${hostName}" or "${guestName}" ("question") to the exact verbatim sentence spoken in response by the counter-party ("answer").
1552 2. "question_by": Must be strictly "${hostName}" or "${guestName}".
1553 3. "answered_by": Must be strictly "${hostName}" or "${guestName}".
1554 4. The question and answer MUST be an actual conversational exchange between different users in the text.
1555 5. Provide an accuracy verdict strictly as one of:
1556    "Accurate" (fully resolved / factual),
1557    "Incomplete" (partially answered or skipped details), or
1558    "Evasive" (changed the topic or failed to provide raw metrics).
1559 6. If no literal question-and-answer exchange exists between different users in the text, return []. Do not invent sample dialogues about sync pipelines or packet loss.
1560 
1561 Return ONLY a valid JSON array matching this exact schema:
1562 [
1563   {
1564     "question_by": "String",
1565     "question": "String",
1566     "answered_by": "String",
1567     "answer": "String",
1568     "verdict": "Accurate | Incomplete | Evasive"
1569   }
1570 ]`;
1571 
1572       const response = await model.generateContent(prompt);
1573       const responseText = response.response.text();
1574       let cleanJson = responseText.trim();
1575       if (cleanJson.startsWith("```json")) {
1576         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```json/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1577       } else if (cleanJson.startsWith("```")) {
1578         cleanJson = cleanJson.replace(/^```/, "").replace(/```$/, "").trim();
1579       }
1580 
1581       const parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJson);
1582       if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
1583         return sanitizeFactCheckAudit(parsed, hostName, guestName);
1584       }
1585     } catch (err) {
1586       console.warn("[auditFactCheckWithGemini] Gemini Fact-check API notice:", err.message);
1587     }
1588   }
1589 
1590   return sanitizeFactCheckAudit([], hostName, guestName);
1591 }
1592 
1593 function sanitizeFactCheckAudit(data, hostName = "Host", guestName = "Participant") {
1594   if (Array.isArray(data)) {
1595     return data
1596       .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object" && item.question && item.answer)
1597       .map((item) => {
1598         let verdict = "Accurate";
1599         const vLower = (item.verdict || "").toLowerCase();
1600         if (vLower.includes("incom")) verdict = "Incomplete";
1601         else if (vLower.includes("evas")) verdict = "Evasive";
1602 
1603         return {
1604           question_by: item.question_by === guestName ? guestName : hostName,
1605           question: String(item.question).trim(),
1606           answered_by: item.answered_by === hostName ? hostName : guestName,
1607           answer: String(item.answer).trim(),
1608           verdict
1609         };
1610       });
1611   }
1612 
1613   return [];
1614 }
1615

1621 exports.auditMeetingFactCheck = onCall(

1638     const rawTranscript = data.rawTranscript || meetingData.rawTranscript;
1639 
1640     let hostName = (data.hostName || meetingData.hostName || "Host").trim();
1641     if (!hostName) hostName = "Host";

1643       (meetingData.participants && (meetingData.participants[0]?.name || meetingData.participants[0])) ||
1644       (meetingData.participantNames && meetingData.participantNames[0]) ||
1645       "Participant";
1646 
1647     const isSingleTurn = (rawTranscript || "").split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0).length <= 1;
1648     const isDummy = (rawTranscript || "").trim() === "Meeting audio session connected and verified." ||
1649                     (rawTranscript || "").includes("Meeting audio session connected and verified.");
1650 
1651     if (!rawTranscript || !rawTranscript.trim() || isSingleTurn || isDummy) {

1672       },
1673       { merge: true }
1674     );
1675 
1676     try {
1677       await db.collection("transcripts").doc(meetingId).set(
1678         {
1679           factCheckAudit,
1680           updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
1681         },
1682         { merge: true }
1683       );
1684     } catch { }
1685 
1686     return {
1687       success: true,
1688       meetingId,
1689       factCheckAudit,
1690       model: "gemini-1.5-flash"
1691     };
1692   }
1693 );

JAVASCRIPT api/rooms.js
129 lines • 4.1 KB
Room management serverless API
   1 // api/rooms.js - Vercel Serverless Function for GuardianEye AI Room & PeerJS Registry
   2 // Fully compatible with Vercel Serverless Node.js runtime and Next.js API routes
   3 
   4 const rooms = global.__guardianeye_rooms || (global.__guardianeye_rooms = new Map());
   5 
   6 export default async function handler(req, res) {
   7   // Enable permissive CORS for frontend Vite client and external devices
   8   res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
   9   res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, OPTIONS');
  10   res.setHeader('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type, Authorization');
  11 
  12   if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
  13     return res.status(200).end();
  14   }
  15 
  16   const { method, query } = req;
  17   const body = req.body || {};
  18   const action = query.action || body.action;
  19   const code = (query.code || body.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  20 
  21   try {
  22     // 1. Create Room (Host)
  23     if (action === 'create' || action === 'create_meeting' || (method === 'POST' && !action && body.hostPeerId)) {
  24       const roomCode = code || (
  25         Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 5).toUpperCase() + '-' +
  26         Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 5).toUpperCase()
  27       );
  28 
  29       const room = {
  30         code: roomCode,
  31         topic: body.topic || 'Strategy Sync',
  32         hostPeerId: body.hostPeerId,
  33         hostName: body.hostName || 'Host',
  34         peers: body.hostPeerId ? [
  35           {
  36             id: body.hostPeerId,
  37             name: body.hostName || 'Host',
  38             role: 'host'
  39           }
  40         ] : [],
  41         maxMembers: 5,
  42         webrtcConfig: {
  43           iceTransportPolicy: 'all',
  44           iceCandidatePoolSize: 10,
  45           maxBitrateKbps: 500,
  46           codecs: ['H264', 'VP8'],
  47           audioPriority: 'high'
  48         },
  49         createdAt: Date.now()
  50       };
  51 
  52       rooms.set(roomCode, room);
  53       return res.status(200).json({ status: 'success', room });
  54     }
  55 
  56     // 2. Get Room Status & Active Peer List
  57     if (method === 'GET' || action === 'get') {
  58       if (!code || !rooms.has(code)) {
  59         return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Room not found. Please check meeting code.' });
  60       }
  61       const room = rooms.get(code);
  62       return res.status(200).json({
  63         status: 'success',
  64         code: room.code,
  65         topic: room.topic,
  66         hostPeerId: room.hostPeerId,
  67         hostName: room.hostName,
  68         peers: room.peers,
  69         webrtcConfig: room.webrtcConfig,
  70         participantCount: room.peers.length
  71       });
  72     }
  73 
  74     // 3. Register Participant Knock / Join
  75     if (action === 'join') {
  76       if (!code || !rooms.has(code)) {
  77         return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Room not found. Please check meeting code.' });
  78       }
  79       const room = rooms.get(code);
  80 
  81       // Max capacity enforcement: Up to 5 members total
  82       if (room.peers.length >= 5) {
  83         return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Room is at full capacity (Maximum 5 members allowed).' });
  84       }
  85 
  86       const { peerId, name } = body;
  87       if (!peerId) {
  88         return res.status(400).json({ error: 'peerId is required to join' });
  89       }
  90 
  91       const existingIndex = room.peers.findIndex(p => p.id === peerId);
  92       if (existingIndex === -1) {
  93         room.peers.push({
  94           id: peerId,
  95           name: name || 'Guest',
  96           role: 'participant'
  97         });
  98       }
  99 
 100       return res.status(200).json({
 101         status: 'success',
 102         room: {
 103           code: room.code,
 104           topic: room.topic,
 105           hostPeerId: room.hostPeerId,
 106           hostName: room.hostName,
 107           peers: room.peers
 108         }
 109       });
 110     }
 111 
 112     // 4. Leave / Unregister Peer
 113     if (action === 'leave') {
 114       if (rooms.has(code)) {
 115         const room = rooms.get(code);
 116         room.peers = room.peers.filter(p => p.id !== body.peerId);
 117         if (room.peers.length === 0) {
 118           rooms.delete(code);
 119         }
 120       }
 121       return res.status(200).json({ status: 'success' });
 122     }
 123 
 124     return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid action or request format' });
 125   } catch (err) {
 126     console.error('[api/rooms] Error:', err);
 127     return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error', details: err.message });
 128   }
 129 }
TEXT firestore.rules

Cloud Firestore security rules
   1 rules_version = '2';
   2 service cloud.firestore {
   3   match /databases/{database}/documents {
   4     
   5     // User profile documents
   6     match /users/{userId} {
   7       allow read, write: if request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == userId;
   8     }
   9 
  10     // Meeting documents
  11     match /meetings/{meetingId} {
  12       // Allow read/write if the meeting belongs to the authenticated user or user is creator
  13       allow read, write: if request.auth != null && 
  14         (resource == null || resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid || request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid);
  15     }
  16 
  17     // Meeting Transcripts collection
  18     // Users can only read and write their own meeting transcript logs
  19     match /transcripts/{transcriptId} {
  20       allow read: if request.auth != null && 
  21         (resource == null || resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid);
  22       allow create, update: if request.auth != null && 
  23         (request.resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid);
  24       allow delete: if request.auth != null && 
  25         resource.data.userId == request.auth.uid;
  26     }
  27 
  28     // Subcollections under meetings
  29     match /meetings/{meetingId}/transcripts/{transcriptDoc} {
  30       allow read, write: if request.auth != null;
  31     }
  32   }
  33 }
TEXT storage.rules
14 lines • 0.4 KB
Firebase Storage security rules
   1 rules_version = '2';
   2 service firebase.storage {
   3   match /b/{bucket}/o {
   4     // Meetings audio/video storage path: meetings/{userId}/{meetingId}.mp4
   5     match /meetings/{userId}/{allPaths=**} {
   6       allow read, write: if request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == userId;
   7     }
   8 
   9     // Recordings general storage path
  10     match /recordings/{allPaths=**} {
  11       allow read, write: if request.auth != null;
  12     }
  13   }
  14 }
